📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期人乳头瘤病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 最常检测到的基因型是 HPV52 (n = 24)、HPV31 (n = 23)、HPV51 (n = 22) 和 HPV56 (n = 22)。另一方面,在宫颈上皮内瘤变 3 (CIN3) 病例中,常见检测到 HPV51、HPV52、HPV59、HPV66 和 HPV31 的组合。慢性宫颈炎患者的中位最低患者水平 Ct 值为 22.0(四分位距 -IQR 8),CIN1 为 19.0(IQR 10),CIN2 为 17.0(IQR 8),CIN3 为 19.0(IQR 7)。
  • 在 16 年的随访中,大多数细胞学和/或组织学证实的低级别和高级别 SIL 与属于 A9 分支的非 疫苗 靶向、中等致癌性 HPV 类型 33/35/52/58 相关。 [10.1002/ijc.70753]。
  • 在 5,000 名接受筛查的女性中,612 名 HR-HPV 呈阳性,相当于 患病率 的总体比例为 12.2%。HPV阳性病例中,单基因型感染占65.7%,多基因型感染占34.3%。HPV16 是最常见的基因型,其次是 HPV31、HPV56、HPV66 和 HPV51。HR-HPV 患病率 在 25-30 岁女性中最高,并随着年龄的增长而下降。多变量分析显示,年龄低于 35 岁、多个性伴侣、吸烟和缺乏事先筛查与 HR-HPV 阳性的几率较高相关。
  • 在 102 份宫颈癌样本中检测到 HPV DNA。在 HPV16、HPV18 和 HPV45 的 E6 和 E7 基因中发现了多个核苷酸取代。其中,14 个为新颖的 突变,12 个已被报道。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • The most frequently detected genotypes were HPV52 (n = 24), HPV31 (n = 23), HPV51 (n = 22) and HPV56 (n = 22).
  • Over the 16‐year follow‐up, most of the cytologically and/or histologically confirmed low‐grade and high‐grade SILs were associated with non‐vaccine‐targeted, medium oncogenicity HPV types 33/35/52/58 belonging to clade A9.
  • Among 5,000 screened women, 612 were HR-HPV positive, corresponding to an overall prevalence of 12.2%.
  • HPV DNA was detected in 102 cervical carcinoma samples. Multiple nucleotide substitutions were identified in both E6 and E7 genes of HPV16, HPV18 and HPV45. Of these, 14 were novel mutations and 12 were already reported.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期人乳头瘤病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 列日大学的研究人员,包括 Michael Herfs、Diane Bruyère 和 Romane Geuzaine,提出了一种新方法,使用人工智能模型来预测 HPV 感染向癌症的进展,而不是依赖单一生物标志物。这是概念和方法论的发展,而不是已确认的 暴发 或事件。
  • 据出版商报道,一项评估 BNT113 加 Pembrolizumab 治疗 HPV16+ HNSCC 的临床试验未能达到 PFS 终点。 [news-efa902c2a2c31571]。
  • 一项研究描述了一种荧光标记 HPV16 病毒样颗粒 (VLP) 的方法,用于实时组装监控和提高产量,并具有颗粒分选的潜力。 [news-8c9762e168df1606]。
  • 一项研究显示,疫苗 未涵盖的 高危型HPV 基因型,特别是 HPV-35,在拉各斯女性的宫颈感染中占主导地位,43.6% 的参与者 HPV-35 检测呈阳性,34.5% 患有多种 高危型HPV 感染。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Human papillomaviruses news developments.
  • Researchers at the University of Liège, including Michael Herfs, Diane Bruyère, and Romane Geuzaine, have proposed a new approach using AI models to predict the progression of HPV infections to cancer, rather than relying on single biomarkers.
  • A clinical trial evaluating BNT113 Plus Pembrolizumab in HPV16+ HNSCC missed the PFS end point, as reported by the publisher. [news-efa902c2a2c31571].
  • A study describes a method to fluorescently tag HPV16 virus-like particles (VLPs) for real-time assembly monitoring and improved yields, with potential for particle sorting. [news-8c9762e168df1606].
  • A study revealed that high-risk HPV genotypes not covered by vaccines, particularly HPV-35, dominate cervical infections in Lagos women, with 43.6% of participants testing positive for HPV-35 and 34.5% having multiple high-risk HPV infections.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: The Journal of dermatological treatment  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-12  |  DOI: 10.1080/09546634.2026.2746272  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 38, 1, 2746272
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

外用大蒜提取物和 20% 鬼臼素溶液对女性生殖器疣 治疗 的疗效比较:一项三盲随机临床试验。

Comparative efficacy of topical garlic extract and 20% podophyllin solution in the treatment of female genital warts: a triple-blind randomized clinical trial.
作者： Mohsen Naseri, Zahra Sarfallah, Mina Saadat, Fatemeh Masaebi, Maryam Damghanian
摘要中文翻译背景:由 人乳头瘤病毒 引起的尖锐湿疣是常见的性传播感染,没有单一疗法可以提供一致的清除效果。大蒜提取物具有 抗病毒药物 和抗增殖特性,并且可以提供低成本的 治疗。目的:比较外用 5% 大蒜提取物和 20% 鬼臼素溶液治疗女性尖锐湿疣的疗效和安全性。方法:74 名女性被随机分配使用 5% 大蒜提取物 (n = 37) 或 20% 鬼臼素 (n = 37),每周使用一次,持续四个星期。使用边际模型(广义估计方程)评估疣的大小和数量。主要结果是平均疣大小的变化,次要结果是疣数量的变化。结果:使用大蒜提取物后,疣的平均大小从 1.61 ± 0.81 毫米减少到 0.60 ± 0.82 毫米,使用鬼臼素时,疣平均大小从 2.96 ± 7.99 毫米减少到 0.62 ± 0.73 毫米。最终评估时的组间 p 值为 0.756;组×时间交互作用的疣大小 p 值为 0.329,计数 p 值为 0.583。在分析的 35 名和 37 名参与者中,分别有 18 名 (51.4%) 和 19 名 (51.4%) 出现完全缓解;23 例 (65.7%) 和 25 例 (67.6%) 出现完全或部分缓解。8 名(21.6%)接受大蒜治疗的参与者分别出现瘙痒和烧灼感。结论:治疗 均减少了疣的大小和数量,且反应率相似。四周的随访排除了对耐久性和复发的评估。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于 人乳头瘤病毒 引起的生殖器疣是常见的性传播感染,没有单一疗法可以提供一致的清除效果,因此比较外用 5% 大蒜提取物与 20% 鬼臼素溶液对患有生殖器疣的女性的疗效和安全性。
设计与对象
在 74 名女性中进行了一项三盲随机临床试验,其中 37 名女性随机接受 5% 大蒜提取物和 37% 至 20% 鬼臼素溶液,每周使用一次,持续四个星期。
核心方法
使用边际模型(广义估计方程)评估疣大小和数量,主要结果是平均疣大小的变化,次要结果是疣数量的变化。
主要结果
使用大蒜提取物后,疣的平均大小从 1.61 ± 0.81 毫米减少到 0.60 ± 0.82 毫米,使用鬼臼素时,疣平均大小从 2.96 ± 7.99 毫米减少到 0.62 ± 0.73 毫米。最终评估时的组间 p 值为 0.756,疣大小的组间交互 p 值为 0.329,计数为 0.583。
解释与创新
治疗 均减少了疣的大小和数量,且有相似的反应率,表明外用大蒜提取物可能是鬼臼素溶液治疗生殖器疣的可行替代品。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,外用大蒜提取物可以为尖锐湿疣提供一种低成本的 治疗 选择,这在资源有限的环境中特别有益。
局限与证据强度
研究设计的局限性包括随访期短,无法评估耐久性和复发率。由于样本量小且缺乏长期随访,证据强度中等。

Comparative efficacy of topical garlic extract and 20% podophyllin solution in the treatment of female genital warts: a triple-blind randomized clinical trial.

Authors: Mohsen Naseri, Zahra Sarfallah, Mina Saadat, Fatemeh Masaebi, Maryam Damghanian
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Genital warts caused by human papillomavirus are common sexually transmitted infections, with no single therapy providing consistent clearance. Garlic extract has antiviral and antiproliferative properties and may offer a low-cost treatment. OBJECTIVE: To compare the efficacy and safety of topical 5% garlic extract with 20% podophyllin solution in women with genital warts. METHODS: Seventy-four women were randomized to 5% garlic extract (n = 37) or 20% podophyllin (n = 37), applied weekly for four weeks. Wart size and count were assessed using marginal models (generalized estimating equations). The primary outcome was change in mean wart size and the secondary outcome was change in wart count. RESULTS: Mean wart size decreased from 1.61 ± 0.81 to 0.60 ± 0.82 mm with garlic extract and from 2.96 ± 7.99 to 0.62 ± 0.73 mm with podophyllin. The between-group p-value at final assessment was 0.756; group × time interaction p values were 0.329 for wart size and 0.583 for count. Among 35 and 37 participants analyzed, complete response occurred in 18 (51.4%) and 19 (51.4%), respectively; complete or partial response occurred in 23 (65.7%) and 25 (67.6%). Itching and burning occurred in 8 (21.6%) garlic-treated participants each. CONCLUSION: Both treatments reduced wart size and count, with similar response rates. Four-week follow-up precluded assessment of durability and recurrence.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
To compare the efficacy and safety of topical 5% garlic extract with 20% podophyllin solution in women with genital warts, given that genital warts caused by human papillomavirus are common sexually transmitted infections, with no single therapy providing consistent clearance.
Design and population
A triple-blind randomized clinical trial was conducted among 74 women, with 37 randomized to 5% garlic extract and 37 to 20% podophyllin solution, applied weekly for four weeks.
Core methods
Wart size and count were assessed using marginal models (generalized estimating equations), with the primary outcome being change in mean wart size and the secondary outcome being change in wart count.
Main results
Mean wart size decreased from 1.61 ± 0.81 to 0.60 ± 0.82 mm with garlic extract and from 2.96 ± 7.99 to 0.62 ± 0.73 mm with podophyllin. Between-group p-value at final assessment was 0.756, and group × time interaction p-values were 0.329 for wart size and 0.583 for count.
Interpretation and novelty
Both treatments reduced wart size and count, with similar response rates, indicating that topical garlic extract may be a viable alternative to podophyllin solution for treating genital warts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that topical garlic extract could offer a low-cost treatment option for genital warts, which could be particularly beneficial in resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study design limitations include the short follow-up period, which precluded assessment of durability and recurrence. The evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size and lack of long-term follow-up.

Journal: Annals of Medicine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-15  |  DOI: 10.1080/07853890.2026.2746866  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 58, 1, 2746866

超越HPV16/18:宫颈癌谱中高危型HPV的基因型分布和周期阈值

Beyond HPV16/18: genotype distribution of high-risk HPV and cycle threshold values in cervical carcinogenesis spectrum
作者： Esra Isci Bostanci, Suheyl Korhan, Selin Yigit, Elif Caliskan, Gulendam Bozdayi, Esra İşçi Bostancı, Soheil KOOHNAVARD, Selin Yiğit, Elif Çalışkan, Gülendam Bozdayı
摘要中文翻译背景:宫颈癌 是一种病毒依赖性妇科恶性肿瘤,根据 GLOBOCAN 2022,宫颈癌 是全球第四大常见女性癌症。尽管这种频率很高,但在发达国家却很少见到这种情况,因为它们实施了宫颈筛查计划,包括 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 测试和称为 cotest 的巴氏涂片测试。尽管实施基于 HPV 的 疫苗 和筛查策略,这种女性癌症的负担仍然很高,几乎可以消除 疫苗 作为一个公共卫生问题。材料和方法:使用 2022 年 1 月至 2023 年 12 月期间在土耳其三级中心妇科门诊接受宫颈采样的 18 岁以上女性的非 16/18 高危人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 基因分型数据和周期阈值 (ct) 值进行回顾性分析。结果:最常检测到的基因型是 HPV52(n = 24)、HPV31 (n = 23)、HPV51 (n = 22) 和 HPV56 (n = 22)。另一方面,在宫颈上皮内瘤变 3 (CIN3) 病例中,常见检测到 HPV51、HPV52、HPV59、HPV66 和 HPV31 的组合。慢性宫颈炎患者的中位最低患者水平 Ct 值为 22.0(四分位距 -IQR 8),CIN1 为 19.0(IQR 10),CIN2 为 17.0(IQR 8),CIN3 为 19.0(IQR 7)。结论:据我们所知,有关预测病毒载量的周期阈值的数据有限。这些区域特异性、基因型解析的数据可以为基于扩展基因分型的风险分层提供信息,并支持基于 HPV 筛查计划中正在进行的非 16/18 HR-HPV 模式的 监测。因此,这项研究为这个时代打开了一个新的视角。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 是一种病毒依赖性妇科恶性肿瘤,根据 GLOBOCAN 2022,宫颈癌 是全球第四大常见女性癌症。尽管这种频率很高,但在发达国家却很少见到这种情况,因为它们实施了宫颈筛查计划,包括 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 测试和称为 cotest 的巴氏涂片测试。
设计与对象
使用 2022 年 1 月至 2023 年 12 月期间在土耳其三级中心妇科门诊接受宫颈采样的 18 岁以上女性的非 16/18 高危人乳头状瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 基因分型数据和周期阈值 (ct) 值进行回顾性分析。
核心方法
该研究对 2022 年 1 月至 2023 年 12 月期间在土耳其一家三级中心收集的宫颈样本的 HR-HPV 基因分型数据和周期阈值 (ct) 进行了回顾性分析。
主要结果
最常检测到的基因型是 HPV52 (n = 24)、HPV31 (n = 23)、HPV51 (n = 22) 和 HPV56 (n = 22)。另一方面,在宫颈上皮内瘤变 3 (CIN3) 病例中,常见检测到 HPV51、HPV52、HPV59、HPV66 和 HPV31 的组合。慢性宫颈炎患者的中位最低患者水平 Ct 值为 22.0(四分位距 -IQR 8),CIN1 为 19.0(IQR 10),CIN2 为 17.0(IQR 8),CIN3 为 19.0(IQR 7)。
解释与创新
该研究深入了解了非 16/18 高危型HPV 基因型的分布及其与宫颈癌发生不同阶段的关联,强调了周期阈值在预测病毒载量方面的潜在作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些区域特异性、基因型解析的数据可以为基于扩展基因分型的风险分层提供信息,并支持基于 HPV 筛查计划中正在进行的非 16/18 HR-HPV 模式的 监测。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于回顾性数据,可能会受到此类设计固有的偏差的影响。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它提供了不同宫颈疾病阶段的详细基因型分布和周期阈值。

Beyond HPV16/18: genotype distribution of high-risk HPV and cycle threshold values in cervical carcinogenesis spectrum

Authors: Esra Isci Bostanci, Suheyl Korhan, Selin Yigit, Elif Caliskan, Gulendam Bozdayi, Esra İşçi Bostancı, Soheil KOOHNAVARD, Selin Yiğit, Elif Çalışkan, Gülendam Bozdayı
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Cervical cancer is a virus-dependent gynaecologic malignancy and according to GLOBOCAN 2022 cervical cancer is the fourth frequent women cancer in all over the world. Despite this frequency it is rarely seen in developed countries because of the enforcement of cervical screening programmes with human papillomavirus (HPV) test and Pap test called cotest. The burden of this women cancer is high despite the implementation of HPV-based vaccines and screening strategies, could nearly eliminate cervical cancer as a public health problem. MATERIALS AND METHODS: A retrospective analysis was conducted using non-16/18 high risk human papilloma virus (HR-HPV) genotyping data and cycle threshold (ct) values from women aged ≥18 years that underwent cervical sampling during gynaecological outpatient clinic at a tertiary centre in Turkey, between January 2022 and December 2023. RESULTS: The most frequently detected genotypes were HPV52 (n = 24), HPV31 (n = 23), HPV51 (n = 22) and HPV56 (n = 22). On the other hand in cervical intraepithelial neoplasia 3 (CIN3) cases, with combinations involving HPV51, HPV52, HPV59, HPV66 and HPV31 commonly detected. Median minimum patient-level Ct values were 22.0 (Interquartile range-IQR 8) in chronic cervicitis, 19.0 (IQR 10) in CIN1, 17.0 (IQR 8) in CIN2 and 19.0 (IQR 7) in CIN3. CONCLUSION: To the best of our knowledge, there are limited data about cycle threshold values in prediction of viral load. These region-specific, genotype-resolved data may inform extended-genotyping-based risk stratification and support ongoing surveillance of non-16/18 HR-HPV patterns within HPV-based screening programs. Hence this study opens a new perspective in this era.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer is a virus-dependent gynaecologic malignancy and according to GLOBOCAN 2022 cervical cancer is the fourth frequent women cancer in all over the world. Despite this frequency it is rarely seen in developed countries because of the enforcement of cervical screening programmes with human papillomavirus (HPV) test and Pap test called cotest.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis was conducted using non-16/18 high risk human papilloma virus (HR-HPV) genotyping data and cycle threshold (ct) values from women aged ≥18 years that underwent cervical sampling during gynaecological outpatient clinic at a tertiary centre in Turkey, between January 2022 and December 2023.
Core methods
The study used retrospective analysis of HR-HPV genotyping data and cycle threshold (ct) values from cervical samples collected in a tertiary centre in Turkey between January 2022 and December 2023.
Main results
The most frequently detected genotypes were HPV52 (n = 24), HPV31 (n = 23), HPV51 (n = 22) and HPV56 (n = 22). On the other hand in cervical intraepithelial neoplasia 3 (CIN3) cases, with combinations involving HPV51, HPV52, HPV59, HPV66 and HPV31 commonly detected. Median minimum patient-level Ct values were 22.0 (Interquartile range-IQR 8) in chronic cervicitis, 19.0 (IQR 10) in CIN1, 17.0 (IQR 8) in CIN2 and 19.0 (IQR 7) in CIN3.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides insights into the distribution of non-16/18 high-risk HPV genotypes and their association with different stages of cervical carcinogenesis, highlighting the potential role of cycle threshold values in predicting viral load.
Scientific and public-health significance
These region-specific, genotype-resolved data may inform extended-genotyping-based risk stratification and support ongoing surveillance of non-16/18 HR-HPV patterns within HPV-based screening programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on retrospective data and may be subject to biases inherent in such designs. However, the evidence is strong as it provides detailed genotype distribution and cycle threshold values across different cervical disease stages.

Journal: International Journal of Cancer  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-15  |  DOI: 10.1002/ijc.70753  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种女性中 宫颈癌 筛查频率的对照试验 II:特定非 疫苗 HPV 类型的宫颈癌前终点

Controlled Trial of Cervical Cancer Screening Frequency Among Human Papillomavirus Vaccinated Women II : Cervical Pre‐Cancer End‐Points by Specific Non‐Vaccine HPV Types
作者： Matti Lehtinen, Mònica Ortega Llobet, Tiina Eriksson, Iacopo Baussano, Miriam Elfström, Mehran Ghaderi, Penelope Grace Gray, Katja Harjula, Kaisa Heikkilä, Mari Hokkanen
摘要中文翻译摘要:我们对 2007 年至 2009 年接种 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 16/18 型疫苗的 5626 名早期青春期女性进行了一项对照试验,以评估高强度与低强度宫颈筛查的影响,分别在 22/25/28 岁和 28 岁,以鳞状细胞上皮内病变 (SIL) 作为终点。在 16 年的随访中,大多数细胞学和/或组织学证实的低级别和高级别 SIL 与属于 A9 分支的非 疫苗 靶向、中等致癌性 HPV 类型 33/35/52/58 相关。在识别这些病变方面,低强度宫颈筛查并不逊色于高强度筛查。可能,从 疫苗 覆盖的 HPV 类型的宫颈感染到非 疫苗 覆盖的 HPV 类型感染的转变有助于更容易控制筛查检测到的宫颈病变的发生。可能有必要将已接种疫苗的女性的宫颈筛查减少到最低限度,以平衡筛查的利弊比。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在识别这些病变方面,低强度宫颈筛查并不逊色于高强度筛查。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
摘要:我们对 2007 年至 2009 年接种 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 16/18 型疫苗的 5626 名早期青春期女性进行了一项对照试验,以评估高强度与低强度宫颈筛查的影响,分别在 22/25/28 岁和 28 岁,以鳞状细胞上皮内病变 (SIL) 作为终点。
主要结果
在 16 年的随访中,大多数细胞学和/或组织学证实的低级别和高级别 SIL 与属于 A9 分支的非 疫苗 靶向、中等致癌性 HPV 类型 33/35/52/58 相关。
解释与创新
可能,从 疫苗 覆盖的 HPV 类型的宫颈感染到非 疫苗 覆盖的 HPV 类型感染的转变有助于更容易控制筛查检测到的宫颈病变的发生。
科研与公卫意义
可能有必要将已接种疫苗的女性的宫颈筛查减少到最低限度,以平衡筛查的利弊比。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取基于检索到的全文证据。消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Controlled Trial of Cervical Cancer Screening Frequency Among Human Papillomavirus Vaccinated Women II : Cervical Pre‐Cancer End‐Points by Specific Non‐Vaccine HPV Types

Authors: Matti Lehtinen, Mònica Ortega Llobet, Tiina Eriksson, Iacopo Baussano, Miriam Elfström, Mehran Ghaderi, Penelope Grace Gray, Katja Harjula, Kaisa Heikkilä, Mari Hokkanen
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT We conducted a controlled trial among 5626 early adolescent women vaccinated in 2007–2009 against human papillomavirus (HPV) types 16/18 to evaluate the impact of high‐intensity versus low‐intensity cervical screening, respectively at ages 22/25/28 and 28 years with squamous‐cell intraepithelial lesions (SILs) as the end‐point. Over the 16‐year follow‐up, most of the cytologically and/or histologically confirmed low‐grade and high‐grade SILs were associated with non‐vaccine‐targeted, medium oncogenicity HPV types 33/35/52/58 belonging to clade A9. The low‐intensity cervical screening was non‐inferior to the high‐intensity screening in the identification of these lesions. Possibly, the transition from cervical infections with vaccine‐covered HPV types to infections with non‐vaccine‐covered HPV types contributes to more manageable occurrence of the screen‐detected cervical lesions. Reduction of cervical screening among vaccinated women to a minimum may be warranted to balance the harm—benefit ratio of screening.

The low‐intensity cervical screening was non‐inferior to the high‐intensity screening in the identification of these lesions.

Main results

乌兹别克斯坦奇尔奇克妇女中 高危型HPV 基因型组的三年持续性和清除模式:试点筛选研究的二次分析

Three-year persistence and clearance patterns of high-risk HPV genotype groups among women in Chirchik, Uzbekistan: A secondary analysis of a pilot screening study
作者： Dilobar Utkirovna Abdumalikova
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 高危型 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 的持续存在是宫颈癌发生的核心,但来自中亚的纵向数据仍然有限。这项二次分析评估了乌兹别克斯坦奇尔奇克女性中 hrHPV 基因型组的分布和 3 年随访模式。方法 2021年8月至2022年3月,对5,678名女性进行了hrHPV筛查,其中547名(9.6%)检测结果呈阳性。使用 Xpert HPV 检测进行 HPV 基因分型。单次感染定义为检测到一个基因型组,而多重感染定义为检测到两个或多个基因型组。清除定义为随访时 HPV16 (P1) 消失,无论其他基因型的状态如何。结果 基线时单一感染占主导地位(87.0%、476/547),而双重、三重和四重感染分别占病例的 11.2%、1.6% 和 0.2%。最常检测到的基因型组是 HPV16 (29.8%)、HPV31/33/35/52/58 (19.9%) 和 HPV39/56/66/68 (16.5%)。在 3 年随访中,409 名基线 hrHPV 阳性女性接受了重新评估,其中 108 名仍为 hrHPV 阳性。在持续性 hrHPV 阳性病例中,单一感染仍然占主导地位(94.4%,102/108)。HPV16是随访时最常检测到的基因型组(38.0%),其次是HPV39/56/66/68(24.1%)和HPV31/33/35/52/58(19.4%)。在 36 名女性中观察到新检测到的基因型组,其中 HPV39/56/66/68 最常见(52.8%)。在基线时感染 HPV16 的女性中,单一 HPV16 感染者的清除率为 66.9% (83/124),HPV16 + HPV31/33/35/52/58 (7/9) 合并感染者为 77.8%,HPV16 + HPV39/56/66/68 (5/6) 合并感染者为 83.3%。Fisher 精确检验显示三组之间的清除率没有统计学上的显着差异 (p = 0.740)。结论 该二次分析为乌兹别克斯坦 hrHPV 基因型组的分布和 3 年随访模式提供了初步证据。需要进行更大规模的研究,包括重复的后续测试和基因型水平分析来证实这些发现。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查乌兹别克斯坦奇里奇克地区女性中高危型HPV(hrHPV)基因型群组在3年期间的持续存在和清除模式,因为目前中亚地区关于hrHPV动态的纵向数据有限。
设计与对象
2021 年 8 月至 2022 年 3 月,对乌兹别克斯坦奇尔奇克的 5,678 名女性进行了一项试点筛查研究的二次分析,其中 547 人的 hrHPV 检测呈阳性。对其中 409 名女性进行了 3 年随访评估。
核心方法
该研究使用Xpert HPV检测进行HPV基因分型,并将单次感染定义为检测一个基因型组,而多重感染定义为检测两个或更多组。清除被定义为随访时 HPV16 消失,无论其他基因型如何。
主要结果
基线时,单一感染占病例的87.0%,双重、三重和四重感染分别占11.2%、1.6%和0.2%。在 3 年随访中,在持续 hrHPV 阳性病例中,单一感染仍然占主导地位,其中 HPV16 是最常检测到的基因型组 (38.0%)。
解释与创新
该研究提供了乌兹别克斯坦 hrHPV 基因型群体的分布及其 3 年随访模式的初步证据,强调了单一感染的优势和 HPV16 的持续存在。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现有助于了解中亚地区的 hrHPV 动态,从而为 监测、诊断 以及 宫颈癌 和相关疾病的预防策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是对试点筛选研究的二次分析,这可能会限制数据的深度和普遍性。由于缺乏重复的后续测试和基因型水平分析,该证据被认为是中等的。

Three-year persistence and clearance patterns of high-risk HPV genotype groups among women in Chirchik, Uzbekistan: A secondary analysis of a pilot screening study

Authors: Dilobar Utkirovna Abdumalikova
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background High-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) persistence is central to cervical carcinogenesis, yet longitudinal data from Central Asia remain limited. This secondary analysis assessed the distribution of hrHPV genotype groups and 3-year follow-up patterns among women in Chirchik, Uzbekistan. Methods From August 2021 to March 2022, 5,678 women were screened for hrHPV, of whom 547 (9.6%) tested positive. HPV genotyping was performed using the Xpert HPV assay. Single infections were defined as the detection of one genotype group, while multiple infections were defined as the detection of two or more groups. Clearance was defined as the loss of HPV16 (P1) at follow-up, regardless of the status of other genotypes. Results Single infections predominated at baseline (87.0%, 476/547), while double, triple, and quadruple infections accounted for 11.2%, 1.6%, and 0.2% of cases, respectively. The most frequently detected genotype groups were HPV16 (29.8%), HPV31/33/35/52/58 (19.9%), and HPV39/56/66/68 (16.5%). At 3-year follow-up, 409 baseline hrHPV-positive women were re-evaluated, and 108 remained hrHPV-positive. Single infections remained predominant among persistent hrHPV-positive cases (94.4%, 102/108). HPV16 was the most frequently detected genotype group at follow-up (38.0%), followed by HPV39/56/66/68 (24.1%) and HPV31/33/35/52/58 (19.4%). Newly detected genotype groups were observed in 36 women, with HPV39/56/66/68 being the most common (52.8%). Among women with HPV16 at baseline, clearance was observed in 66.9% (83/124) of those with single HPV16 infection, 77.8% of those with HPV16 + HPV31/33/35/52/58 (7/9) co-infection, and 83.3% of HPV16 + HPV39/56/66/68 (5/6) co-infection. A Fisher’s exact test showed no statistically significant difference in clearance proportions between the three groups (p = 0.740). Conclusion This secondary analysis provides preliminary evidence on the distribution of hrHPV genotype groups and 3-year follow-up patterns in Uzbekistan. Larger studies with repeated follow-up testing and genotype-level analysis are needed to confirm these findings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the persistence and clearance patterns of high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) genotype groups over a 3-year period among women in Chirchik, Uzbekistan, as longitudinal data from Central Asia on hrHPV dynamics are limited.
Design and population
A secondary analysis of a pilot screening study was conducted among 5,678 women in Chirchik, Uzbekistan, from August 2021 to March 2022, with 547 testing positive for hrHPV. Follow-up assessments were performed on 409 of these women at 3 years.
Core methods
The study used the Xpert HPV assay for HPV genotyping and defined single infections as the detection of one genotype group, while multiple infections were defined as the detection of two or more groups. Clearance was defined as the loss of HPV16 at follow-up, regardless of other genotypes.
Main results
At baseline, single infections accounted for 87.0% of cases, while double, triple, and quadruple infections accounted for 11.2%, 1.6%, and 0.2%, respectively. At 3-year follow-up, single infections remained predominant among persistent hrHPV-positive cases, with HPV16 being the most frequently detected genotype group (38.0%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides preliminary evidence on the distribution of hrHPV genotype groups and their 3-year follow-up patterns in Uzbekistan, highlighting the predominance of single infections and the persistence of HPV16.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings contribute to understanding hrHPV dynamics in Central Asia, which can inform targeted surveillance, diagnosis, and prevention strategies for cervical cancer and related diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a secondary analysis of a pilot screening study, which may limit the depth of data and generalisability. The evidence is considered moderate due to the lack of repeated follow-up testing and genotype-level analysis.

人乳头瘤病毒 L1 衣壳蛋白的组装途径以静电相互作用编码

The assembly pathway for human papillomavirus L1 capsid protein is encoded in electrostatic interactions
作者： X. Yuan, Kripa N. Nand, J. C. Patel, A. Jin, C. Bystroff
摘要中文翻译摘要 人乳头瘤病毒 L1 蛋白在高盐浓度下自发折叠并组装成 60 nm 病毒样颗粒(VLP),具有 T = 7d 二十面体对称性,但在低盐浓度下则不然。在组装过程中从低盐到高盐的快速增加会产生质量较差的 VLP,而在长达 48 小时内缓慢增加盐会产生高质量的 VLP。对这种行为的深入了解是通过基于 人乳头瘤病毒 16 型 VLP 低温电子显微镜 (cryo-EM) 结构的计算模型得出的。使用线性化泊松-玻尔兹曼方程计算静电势能垒。在单体最初快速寡聚成对称五聚体(壳粒)后,这些单元以能量顺序形成成对相互作用,最低势垒首先。但在高盐浓度下,无论是在模拟还是在实验中,能量势垒或多或少都是相等的,并且粒子形式不规则。相互作用界面由 突变 修改,以支持更小的 30 nm T = 1 二十面体对称性,而不支持 T = 7d 对称性。结果是更小的颗粒。通过仔细控制离子强度和/或通过设计突变界面残基,可以直接从未纯化的细菌裂解物中制备高质量、高免疫原性的VLP,而不需要使用拆卸/重新组装步骤。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) L1 衣壳蛋白的组装途径以及盐浓度如何影响病毒样颗粒 (VLP) 的形成。这对于为 疫苗 应用开发高质量的 VLP 非常重要。
设计与对象
该研究使用了基于 人乳头瘤病毒 16 型 VLP 低温电子显微镜 (cryo-EM) 结构的计算模型。它涉及模拟不同盐浓度下的组装过程并分析所得结构。
核心方法
研究人员使用线性化泊松-玻尔兹曼方程计算静电势能垒。他们还通过 突变 修改了交互界面以支持特定的对称性,并观察了这些变化的结果。
主要结果
结果表明,当盐浓度快速增加时,会形成质量较差的VLP,而在48小时内缓慢增加会形成高质量的VLP。发现 突变 更倾向于较小的 30 nm T = 1 二十面体对称性,而不是 T = 7d 对称性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,组装途径受静电相互作用控制,控制离子强度或突变特定残基可以导致直接从细菌裂解物中形成高质量、免疫原性的 VLP。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更有效的方法来生产 疫苗 的 HPV VLP 提供信息,从而有可能消除拆卸和重新组装步骤的需要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于计算模型,不包括超出初始观察结果的实验​​验证。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于模拟和受控的 突变。

The assembly pathway for human papillomavirus L1 capsid protein is encoded in electrostatic interactions

Authors: X. Yuan, Kripa N. Nand, J. C. Patel, A. Jin, C. Bystroff
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Human papillomavirus L1 protein spontaneously folds and assembles into 60 nm virus‐like particles (VLP), with T = 7d icosahedral symmetry at high salt concentrations but not in low salt. Rapid increase from low to high salt during the assembly process gives poor‐quality VLPs, while slowly increasing salt, over as many as 48 h, gives rise to high‐quality VLPs. Insight into this behavior is drawn from a computational model based on the cryogenic electron microscopy (cryo‐EM) structure of the human papillomavirus Type 16 VLP. Electrostatic potential energy barriers were calculated using the linearized Poisson–Boltzmann equation. After initial, rapid oligomerization of monomers into symmetrical pentamers (capsomeres), these units form pairwise interactions in energetic order, lowest barrier first. But at high salt concentrations, the energetic barriers are more or less equal and irregular particles form, both in simulation and in experiments. The interaction interfaces were modified by mutation to favor the smaller, 30 nm T = 1 icosahedral symmetry and disfavor T = 7d symmetry. The results were smaller particles. By carefully controlling ionic strength, and/or by mutating interface residues by design, it is possible to make high quality, highly immunogenic VLPs directly from unpurified bacterial lysate, without requiring the use of a disassembly/reassembly step.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the assembly pathway of human papillomavirus (HPV) L1 capsid protein and how salt concentration affects the formation of virus-like particles (VLPs). This is important for developing high-quality VLPs for vaccine applications.
Design and population
The study used a computational model based on the cryogenic electron microscopy (cryo-EM) structure of the human papillomavirus Type 16 VLP. It involved simulating the assembly process under varying salt concentrations and analyzing the resulting structures.
Core methods
The researchers calculated electrostatic potential energy barriers using the linearized Poisson–Boltzmann equation. They also modified interaction interfaces through mutation to favor specific symmetries and observed the outcomes of these changes.
Main results
The results showed that when salt concentration increases rapidly, poor-quality VLPs form, while a slow increase over 48 hours leads to high-quality VLPs. Mutations were found to favor smaller, 30 nm T = 1 icosahedral symmetry over T = 7d symmetry.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the assembly pathway is governed by electrostatic interactions and that controlling ionic strength or mutating specific residues can lead to the formation of high-quality, immunogenic VLPs directly from bacterial lysate.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform the development of more efficient methods for producing HPV VLPs for vaccines, potentially eliminating the need for disassembly and reassembly steps.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to computational models and does not include experimental validation beyond the initial observations. The evidence is moderate as it relies on simulations and controlled mutations.

基于正念的咨询对 人乳头瘤病毒 女性羞耻感的影响

The effect of mindfulness-based counseling on shame in women with human papillomavirus
作者： Sara Hosseini, Azam Maleki, Omid Saed, Hamideh Gholami, Mina Abbasi
摘要中文翻译这项研究评估了基于正念的咨询对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 女性羞耻感的影响,解决了标准化心理健康支持方面的差距以及 HPV 护理中经常被忽视的情绪困扰。这项随机对照试验于 2024 年在伊朗赞詹 Ayatollah Mousavi 医院进行。参与者是在例行访视期间招募的,并使用分组随机化随机分配到干预组或对照组(每组 70 人)。干预组接受标准护理以及每周 8 次为期 90 分钟的正念团体咨询课程(1 次面对面咨询,7 次虚拟咨询)。对照组接受规范的医疗护理和健康教育。使用Goss等人的问卷调查来测量外部羞耻感。使用独立样本和配对样本 t 检验以及基线调整的 ANCOVA 分析数据,p < 0.05 被认为具有统计显着性。组间没有观察到显着的基线差异。对基线外部羞耻进行调整后,ANCOVA 显示干预组的干预后评分显着低于对照组(F = 56.03,p < 0.001,部分 η2 = 0.292)。配对样本 t 检验显示干预组 (p < 0.001) 和对照组 (p = 0.011) (p < 0.001) 组内均显着降低。这些发现表明,基于正念的咨询可能有潜力成为 HPV 护理的支持性心理社会组成部分。需要进行更长随访期和文化敏感方法的进一步研究,以检验这些研究结果的持久性及其在不同医疗保健环境中的适用性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究评估了基于正念的咨询对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 女性羞耻感的影响,解决了标准化心理健康支持方面的差距以及 HPV 护理中经常被忽视的情绪困扰。
设计与对象
这项随机对照试验于 2024 年在伊朗赞詹 Ayatollah Mousavi 医院进行,干预组和对照组各有 70 名参与者。
核心方法
参与者被随机分配接受标准护理以及每周 8 次为期 90 分钟的正念团体咨询课程(1 次面对面咨询,7 次虚拟咨询)或标准医疗护理和健康教育。
主要结果
对基线外部羞耻进行调整后,ANCOVA 显示干预组的干预后评分显着低于对照组(F = 56.03,p < 0.001,部分 η2 = 0.292)。配对样本 t 检验显示干预组 (p < 0.001) 和对照组 (p = 0.011) 的组内显着下降。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,基于正念的咨询可能有潜力成为 HPV 护理的支持性心理社会组成部分。
科研与公卫意义
需要进行更长随访期和文化敏感方法的进一步研究,以检验这些研究结果的持久性及其在不同医疗保健环境中的适用性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其较短的随访期以及需要进行更长期的研究来确认研究结果的持久性。样本量足以满足研究设计的需要,但可能无法推广到所有人群。

The effect of mindfulness-based counseling on shame in women with human papillomavirus

Authors: Sara Hosseini, Azam Maleki, Omid Saed, Hamideh Gholami, Mina Abbasi
Original abstract (English)
This study evaluated the impact of mindfulness-based counseling on shame in women with Human Papillomavirus (HPV), addressing the gap in standardized mental health support and the often-overlooked emotional distress in HPV care. This randomized controlled trial was conducted in 2024 at Ayatollah Mousavi Hospital in Zanjan, Iran. Participants were recruited during routine visits and randomly assigned to intervention or control groups (70 per group) using block randomization. The intervention group received standard care plus eight weekly 90-minute mindfulness-based group counseling sessions (one in-person, seven virtual). The control group received standard medical care and health education. External shame was measured using Goss et al.'s Questionnaire. Data were analysed using independent- and paired-samples t-tests and baseline-adjusted ANCOVA, with p < 0.05 considered statistically significant. No significant baseline difference was observed between groups. After adjustment for baseline external shame, ANCOVA showed significantly lower post-intervention scores in the intervention group than in the control group (F = 56.03, p < 0.001, partial η2 = 0.292). Paired-samples t-tests showed significant within-group reductions in both the intervention (p < 0.001) and control (p = 0.011) groups (p < 0.001). These findings suggest that mindfulness-based counseling may have potential as a supportive psychosocial component of HPV care. Further research with longer follow-up periods and culturally sensitive approaches is needed to examine the durability of these findings and their applicability across diverse healthcare settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study evaluated the impact of mindfulness-based counseling on shame in women with Human Papillomavirus (HPV), addressing the gap in standardized mental health support and the often-overlooked emotional distress in HPV care.
Design and population
This randomized controlled trial was conducted in 2024 at Ayatollah Mousavi Hospital in Zanjan, Iran, with 70 participants in each of the intervention and control groups.
Core methods
Participants were randomly assigned to receive standard care plus eight weekly 90-minute mindfulness-based group counseling sessions (one in-person, seven virtual) or standard medical care and health education.
Main results
After adjustment for baseline external shame, ANCOVA showed significantly lower post-intervention scores in the intervention group than in the control group (F = 56.03, p < 0.001, partial η2 = 0.292). Paired-samples t-tests showed significant within-group reductions in both the intervention (p < 0.001) and control (p = 0.011) groups.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that mindfulness-based counseling may have potential as a supportive psychosocial component of HPV care.
Scientific and public-health significance
Further research with longer follow-up periods and culturally sensitive approaches is needed to examine the durability of these findings and their applicability across diverse healthcare settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its short follow-up period and the need for longer-term studies to confirm the durability of the findings. The sample size was adequate for the study design but may not be generalizable to all populations.

阴茎淋巴上皮瘤样癌:人乳头瘤病毒 相关阴茎癌的罕见变体。

Penile lymphoepithelioma-like carcinoma: a rare variant of human papillomavirus-associated penile cancer.
作者： Jeremy Cheng, Hui Sien Tay, Justin Chee, Justin B. L. Chee
摘要中文翻译阴茎淋巴上皮瘤样癌 (LELC) 是鳞状细胞癌 (SCC) 的一种极其罕见的变体,之前仅报道过 5 例病例。我们描述了一名 70 多岁的男性,其阴茎偶然出现 3 厘米病变。初始活检显示高级别阴茎上皮内瘤变,随后的部分龟头切除术显示低分化的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关 SCC,具有 LELC 特征,p16 和 p40 免疫组织化学染色阳性。随后,他接受了完整的龟头切除术、部分阴茎切除术和双侧前哨淋巴结活检,结果显示局部中度分化的鳞状细胞癌,没有 LELC 的特征。术后 24 个月,他仍然没有复发。虽然被归类为 HPV 相关病例,但三分之一的报告病例为 p16 阴性,这增加了其 发病机制 的不确定性。在有限数量的描述病例中,阴茎 LELC 似乎与其他阴茎 SCC 亚型具有相似的预后,治疗应遵循当前的现有指南。

Penile lymphoepithelioma-like carcinoma: a rare variant of human papillomavirus-associated penile cancer.

Authors: Jeremy Cheng, Hui Sien Tay, Justin Chee, Justin B. L. Chee
Original abstract (English)
Penile lymphoepithelioma-like carcinoma (LELC) is an extremely rare variant of squamous cell carcinoma (SCC), with only five previously reported cases. We describe a man in his late 70s with an incidental 3 cm penile lesion. Initial biopsy revealed high-grade penile intraepithelial neoplasia and subsequent partial glansectomy demonstrated poorly differentiated human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated SCC with features of LELC, with positive p16 and p40 immunohistochemical stain. He then underwent completion glansectomy, partial penectomy and bilateral sentinel lymph node biopsy, which showed localised moderately differentiated SCC without features of LELC. At 24 months post-surgery, he remains recurrence-free. While classified as HPV-associated, one-third of reported cases are p16-negative, raising uncertainty about its pathogenesis. In the limited number of described cases, penile LELC appears to have a similar prognosis to other penile SCC subtypes and management should follow current existing guidelines.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
We describe a man in his late 70s with an incidental 3 cm penile lesion.
Design and population
Penile lymphoepithelioma-like carcinoma (LELC) is an extremely rare variant of squamous cell carcinoma (SCC), with only five previously reported cases.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results
While classified as HPV-associated, one-third of reported cases are p16-negative, raising uncertainty about its pathogenesis.
Interpretation and novelty
He then underwent completion glansectomy, partial penectomy and bilateral sentinel lymph node biopsy, which showed localised moderately differentiated SCC without features of LELC.
Scientific and public-health significance
In the limited number of described cases, penile LELC appears to have a similar prognosis to other penile SCC subtypes and management should follow current existing guidelines.
Limitations and evidence strength

对 Sirajganj 埃纳耶特普尔选定女子学校青春期女孩 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染的了解

Knowledge about Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Infection among Adolescent Girls in a Selected Girls School in Enayetpur, Sirajganj
作者： Mst Baishakhee Akter, Md Mahmudul Haque, Asma Khatun
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是一种常见的性传播感染,也是全球 宫颈癌 的主要原因。在孟加拉国,宫颈癌 仍然是一个主要的公共卫生问题,引入 HPV 疫苗接种 是为了减轻疾病负担。尽管有 疫苗接种 计划,青少年对 HPV 感染和预防的认识和知识仍然存在差异。目标:评估孟加拉国西拉杰甘杰省 Enayetpur 选定女子学校的青春期女孩对 HPV 感染、其 传播、相关疾病和预防措施(包括 疫苗接种)的了解。材料和方法:2024 年 7 月至 12 月,在 Enayetpur 的 Meher-Un-Nessa 女子高中进行了一项描述性横断面研究。通过便利抽样,共选择了 VIII、IX 和 X 班的 130 名学生。使用半结构化问卷通过面对面访谈收集数据,并使用 SPSS 27 版进行分析。使用表格和图表呈现调查结果。结果:大多数受访者年龄在 14-15 岁(83.8%),并且主要来自核心家庭(68.5%)。大多数受访者 (77.7%) 正确地将 宫颈癌 识别为 HPV 相关疾病,但也存在误解,例如 67.7% 的受访者错误地提到了感冒和咳嗽。关于HPV 传播,52.3%的人提到了性接触,而66.2%的人提到了身体接触。对 HPV 疫苗 的认知度很高 (97.7%),但只有 41.5% 的人报告已接受过该病毒。在预防方面,84.6%的人认可“疫苗接种”,76.2%的人提到健康教育,50.0%的人提到安全行为,而17.7%的人不知道预防措施。结论:该校青春期女生对HPV感染和传播的认知程度较好,但在传播的知识和预防行为方面存在差距。建议开展有针对性的学校健康教育计划,强调准确信息和 疫苗 的吸收,以减少孟加拉国未来与 HPV 相关的疾病负担。京城2026;07(01):12-16
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估孟加拉国少女对 HPV 感染的了解,解决了解认识水平方面的知识差距以及 传播 和预防措施方面的知识差距。这项研究需要为有针对性的健康教育计划提供信息,以减少未来与 HPV 相关的疾病负担。
设计与对象
2024 年 7 月至 12 月,在 Enayetpur Meher-Un-Nessa 女子高中进行了一项描述性横断面研究,涉及 VIII、IX 和 X 年级的 130 名学生,通过便利抽样进行选择。
核心方法
使用半结构化问卷通过面对面访谈收集数据,并使用 SPSS 27 版进行分析。
主要结果
大多数受访者 (83.8%) 年龄在 14-15 岁,来自核心家庭 (68.5%)。大多数受访者 (77.7%) 正确地将 宫颈癌 识别为 HPV 相关疾病,但存在误解。对于传播,52.3%的人提到了性接触,而66.2%的人提到了身体接触。对 HPV 疫苗 的认知度很高 (97.7%),但只有 41.5% 的人接受过该疫苗。在预防方面,84.6%的人认可“疫苗接种”,76.2%的人提到健康教育,50.0%的人提到安全行为,而17.7%的人不知道预防措施。
解释与创新
研究发现,青春期女孩对 HPV 感染和 传播 的认识合理,但强调了在 传播 和预防行为方面的知识差距。研究结果表明需要有针对性的基于学校的健康教育计划。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为制定有针对性的健康教育计划提供信息,以提高对 HPV 疫苗接种 的认识和吸收,从而减少孟加拉国未来与 HPV 相关的疾病负担。
局限与证据强度
该研究使用便利抽样可能会引入选择偏差,并且研究结果可能无法推广到孟加拉国更广泛的青春期女孩群体。证据强度中等。

Knowledge about Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Infection among Adolescent Girls in a Selected Girls School in Enayetpur, Sirajganj

Authors: Mst Baishakhee Akter, Md Mahmudul Haque, Asma Khatun
Original abstract (English)
Background: Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a common sexually transmitted infection and the leading cause of cervical cancer globally. In Bangladesh, cervical cancer remains a major public health concern, and HPV vaccination has been introduced to reduce disease burden. Despite vaccination programs, awareness and knowledge about HPV infection and prevention among adolescents remain variable. Objective(s): To assess the knowledge about HPV infection, its transmission, associated diseases, and preventive measures, including vaccination, among adolescent girls in a selected girls’ school in Enayetpur, Sirajganj, Bangladesh. Materials and methods: A descriptive cross-sectional study was conducted at Meher-Un-Nessa Girls’ High School, Enayetpur, from July to December 2024. A total of 130 students from classes VIII, IX, and X were selected using convenience sampling. Data were collected through face-to-face interviews using a semi-structured questionnaire and analyzed using SPSS version 27. Findings were presented using tables and graphs. Results: The majority of respondents were 14–15 years old (83.8%) and predominantly from nuclear families (68.5%). Most respondents (77.7%) correctly identified cervical cancer as an HPV-related disease, though misconceptions were present, such as 67.7% incorrectly mentioning cold and cough. Regarding HPV transmission, 52.3% identified sexual contact, while 66.2% mentioned physical touch. Awareness of the HPV vaccine was high (97.7%), but only 41.5% reported having received it. Concerning prevention, 84.6% recognized vaccination, 76.2% mentioned health education, and 50.0% cited safe behavior, while 17.7% did not know preventive measures. Conclusion: Adolescent girls in this school had reasonable awareness of HPV infection and vaccination but demonstrated gaps in knowledge regarding transmission and preventive behaviors. Targeted school-based health education programs emphasizing accurate information and vaccine uptake are recommended to reduce future HPV-related disease burden in Bangladesh. KYANCJ 2026; 07 (01): 12-16
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess knowledge about HPV infection among adolescent girls in Bangladesh, addressing a knowledge gap in understanding awareness levels and gaps in knowledge regarding transmission and preventive measures. The study was needed to inform targeted health education programs to reduce future HPV-related disease burden.
Design and population
A descriptive cross-sectional study was conducted at Meher-Un-Nessa Girls’ High School, Enayetpur, from July to December 2024, involving 130 students from classes VIII, IX, and X, selected using convenience sampling.
Core methods
Data were collected through face-to-face interviews using a semi-structured questionnaire and analyzed using SPSS version 27.
Main results
The majority of respondents (83.8%) were 14–15 years old and from nuclear families (68.5%). Most respondents (77.7%) correctly identified cervical cancer as an HPV-related disease, but misconceptions were present. Regarding transmission, 52.3% identified sexual contact, while 66.2% mentioned physical touch. Awareness of the HPV vaccine was high (97.7%), but only 41.5% had received it. Concerning prevention, 84.6% recognized vaccination, 76.2% mentioned health education, and 50.0% cited safe behavior, while 17.7% did not know preventive measures.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found reasonable awareness of HPV infection and vaccination among adolescent girls but highlighted gaps in knowledge regarding transmission and preventive behaviors. The findings suggest the need for targeted school-based health education programs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of targeted health education programs to improve awareness and uptake of HPV vaccination, thereby reducing future HPV-related disease burden in Bangladesh.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's use of convenience sampling may introduce selection bias, and the findings may not be generalizable to the broader population of adolescent girls in Bangladesh. The evidence strength is moderate.

人乳头瘤病毒 患病率 和越南湄公河三角洲已婚女性社区样本的基因型分布:一项多阶段横断面研究

Human papillomavirus prevalence and genotype distribution in a community-based sample of ever-married women in the Mekong Delta, Vietnam: a multistage cross-sectional study
作者： Linh My Duong, Nghia Quang Bui, Trang Huynh Vo, Cua Thi Hong Trinh, Kien Minh Truong, Ngoc My Le, Hien Thi Nguyen, T. H. Võ, Của Thị Hồng Trịnh, Kiển Minh Trương
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
总体而言,78 种 (91.8%) 携带至少一种制造商指定的高风险基因型,66 种 (77.6%) 携带 IARC 第 1 组致癌基因型。
设计与对象
方法 2026 年 1 月至 6 月,我们对越南金瓯省 1,193 名 25-65 岁的已婚女性进行了一项基于社区的横断面研究;答复率分母为 1,565 名妇女,答复率为 76.2%。
核心方法
通过实时PCR检测宫颈标本的23种HPV基因型,同时进行宫颈细胞学检查。
主要结果
在 25/85 HPV 阳性女性 (29.4%) 中检测到多种基因型。
解释与创新

科研与公卫意义
如果没有纵向证据将个体类型与组织学证实的癌前期和癌症联系起来,基因型频率不应转化为当地分诊政策。
局限与证据强度
由于无法获得特定阶段的选择概率,因此这些是研究样本的未加权描述性估计,而不是金瓯省基于设计的估计。

Human papillomavirus prevalence and genotype distribution in a community-based sample of ever-married women in the Mekong Delta, Vietnam: a multistage cross-sectional study

Authors: Linh My Duong, Nghia Quang Bui, Trang Huynh Vo, Cua Thi Hong Trinh, Kien Minh Truong, Ngoc My Le, Hien Thi Nguyen, T. H. Võ, Của Thị Hồng Trịnh, Kiển Minh Trương
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Community-based HPV genotype data remain limited in southern Vietnam, where screening programmes must interpret genotype-specific risk in a population with low documented HPV vaccination. We described HPV prevalence, IARC Group 1 carcinogenic genotype distribution, infection multiplicity, and woman-level detection of HPV16/18 and of at least one genotype included in the nonavalent vaccine. Methods From January to June 2026, we conducted a community-based cross-sectional study among 1,193 enrolled ever-married women aged 25–65 years in Ca Mau Province, Vietnam; the response-rate denominator was 1,565 women, yielding a response rate of 76.2%. A three-stage sampling design with random selection procedures was used. Because stage-specific selection probabilities were unavailable, prevalence estimates are reported as unweighted descriptive proportions. Cervical specimens were tested by real-time PCR for 23 HPV genotypes, and cervical cytology was performed concurrently. Crude proportions and Wilson 95% confidence intervals (CIs) calculated under a simple-random-sampling assumption are reported; no inferential comparisons were performed. Results Crude, unweighted HPV prevalence was 7.1% (85/1,193; 95% CI 5.8–8.7); all 85 had a retrievable genotype result. Overall, 78 (91.8%) carried at least one manufacturer-designated high-risk genotype and 66 (77.6%) carried an IARC Group 1 carcinogenic genotype. HPV52 (20/85, 23.5%) and HPV58 (14/85, 16.5%) were the most frequently detected genotypes, followed by HPV16, HPV51 and HPV59 (each 10/85, 11.8%) and HPV18 (4/85, 4.7%); genotype frequencies were not compared statistically. Multiple genotypes were detected in 25/85 HPV-positive women (29.4%). Among women with an IARC Group 1 genotype, 52/66 (78.8%) had neither HPV16 nor HPV18. At least one nonavalent-vaccine genotype was detected in 50/85 HPV-positive women (58.8%). Conclusions Overall HPV prevalence was low, HPV52 and HPV58 were the most frequently detected genotypes, and nearly four in five women with an IARC Group 1 carcinogenic genotype carried neither HPV16 nor HPV18. Because stage-specific selection probabilities were unavailable, these are unweighted descriptive estimates for the study sample rather than design-based estimates for Ca Mau Province. They describe the baseline genotype distribution in a population with low documented HPV vaccination. Genotype frequency should not be translated into local triage policy without longitudinal evidence linking individual types to histologically confirmed precancer and cancer.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Overall, 78 (91.8%) carried at least one manufacturer-designated high-risk genotype and 66 (77.6%) carried an IARC Group 1 carcinogenic genotype.
Design and population
Methods From January to June 2026, we conducted a community-based cross-sectional study among 1,193 enrolled ever-married women aged 25–65 years in Ca Mau Province, Vietnam; the response-rate denominator was 1,565 women, yielding a response rate of 76.2%.
Core methods
Cervical specimens were tested by real-time PCR for 23 HPV genotypes, and cervical cytology was performed concurrently.
Main results
Multiple genotypes were detected in 25/85 HPV-positive women (29.4%).
Interpretation and novelty
Conclusions Overall HPV prevalence was low, HPV52 and HPV58 were the most frequently detected genotypes, and nearly four in five women with an IARC Group 1 carcinogenic genotype carried neither HPV16 nor HPV18.
Scientific and public-health significance
Genotype frequency should not be translated into local triage policy without longitudinal evidence linking individual types to histologically confirmed precancer and cancer.
Limitations and evidence strength
Because stage-specific selection probabilities were unavailable, these are unweighted descriptive estimates for the study sample rather than design-based estimates for Ca Mau Province.

尼日利亚拉各斯宫颈细胞学检查正常和异常的女性中高危 人乳头瘤病毒 基因型及相关危险因素的分布

Distribution of high-risk human papillomavirus genotypes and associated risk factors among women with normal and abnormal cervical cytology in Lagos, Nigeria
作者： Oluwaseyi Sedowhe Ashaka, Adesuyi Ayodeji Omoare, Bamidele Soji Oderinde, Kehinde Sharafadeen Okunade, Ayorinde Babatunde James, Femi Oladiji, Kayode Adebamiji Adeniji, Olajide Olubunmi Agbede, Adekunbiola Aina Fehintola Banjo, Kehinde Oluwasegun Aina
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 是全球女性中第四大常见癌症,主要由高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 基因型持续感染引起。本研究旨在确定尼日利亚拉各斯宫颈细胞学检查正常和异常的女性的 HR-HPV 基因型分布和相关危险因素。
设计与对象
2017 年 7 月至 2018 年 6 月期间,从三级妇科转诊诊所招募了总共 165 名性活跃女性。在 165 名参与者中,93 名参与者 (56.4%) 对 13 种已确定的 高危型HPV 基因型中的至少一种呈阳性,而 116 名 (70.3%) 对 18 种测试的已确定或可能的基因型中的至少一种呈阳性高危型HPV 基因型。
核心方法
使用巴氏涂片评估宫颈细胞学,而使用针对 18 个 HR-HPV 基因型的巢式多重聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 检测 HR-HPV 并进行基因分型。通过问卷收集社会人口学和临床数据,并使用 SPSS 21 版进行分析。使用卡方和逻辑回归分析评估 HR-HPV 患病率 与危险因素之间的关联。随后使用多变量逻辑回归和 Firth 惩罚似然法确定了与 HR-HPV 感染独立相关的因素。
主要结果
性传播感染史与高危型HPV感染显著相关(OR 2.97;95% CI 1.43–6.17;P = 0.003);较晚的首次性行为(≥21岁)以及更高的终生性伴侣数量显示出升高但未达统计学显著的比值比。多变量校正后,使用安全套(校正后比值比 [AOR] 0.19;95% CI 0.08–0.45;P < 0.001)和性传播感染史(AOR 3.27;95% CI 1.47–7.25;P = 0.004)仍然与高危型HPV感染独立相关。
解释与创新
这项研究揭示了拉各斯女性的 HR-HPV 负担较高,其中大部分基因型未被当前 HPV 疫苗 涵盖。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调迫切需要针对特定​​区域的 疫苗 策略、将分子诊断纳入常规筛查以及有针对性的公共卫生干预措施以减少 HPV 疾病。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的局限性或证据的强度。

Distribution of high-risk human papillomavirus genotypes and associated risk factors among women with normal and abnormal cervical cytology in Lagos, Nigeria

Authors: Oluwaseyi Sedowhe Ashaka, Adesuyi Ayodeji Omoare, Bamidele Soji Oderinde, Kehinde Sharafadeen Okunade, Ayorinde Babatunde James, Femi Oladiji, Kayode Adebamiji Adeniji, Olajide Olubunmi Agbede, Adekunbiola Aina Fehintola Banjo, Kehinde Oluwasegun Aina
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer is the fourth most common cancer in women worldwide and is primarily driven by persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) genotypes. This study aimed to determine HR-HPV genotype distribution and associated risk factors in women with normal and abnormal cervical cytology in Lagos, Nigeria. A total of 165 sexually active women were recruited from a tertiary gynecology referral clinic between July 2017 and June 2018. Cervical cytology was assessed using Pap smears, whereas HR-HPV was detected and genotyped using nested and multiplex polymerase chain reaction (PCR) targeting 18 HR-HPV genotypes (13 established and 5 probable high-risk types). Sociodemographic and clinical data were collected via questionnaires and analyzed using SPSS version 21. Associations between HR-HPV prevalence and risk factors were evaluated using chi-square and logistic regression analyses. Factors independently associated with HR-HPV infection were subsequently identified using multivariable logistic regression with Firth’s penalized-likelihood method. Among the 165 participants, 93 (56.4%) were positive for at least one of the 13 established high-risk HPV genotypes, while 116 (70.3%) were positive for at least one of the 18 tested established or probable high-risk HPV genotypes, with HPV-35 emerging as the predominant genotype (43.6%), followed by HPV-82 (20.0%), and HPV-73 (9.1%). Among women with abnormal cytology (21.8%), HPV-35 was the most frequent genotype across all cytological grades. Dual and multiple HR-HPV infections were common, occurring in 34.5% of all participants. History of sexually transmitted infection was significantly associated with HR-HPV infection (OR, 2.97; 95% CI 1.43–6.17; P = 0.003); later sexual debut (≥ 21 years) and a higher number of lifetime sexual partners showed elevated but not statistically significant odds ratios. After multivariable adjustment, condom use (adjusted odds ratio [AOR], 0.19; 95% CI 0.08–0.45; P < 0.001) and history of sexually transmitted infection (AOR, 3.27; 95% CI 1.47–7.25; P = 0.004) remained independently associated with HR-HPV infection, whereas age, educational status, and marital status did not. This study revealed a high burden of HR-HPV among women in Lagos, with a predominance of genotypes not covered by current HPV vaccines. These findings underscore the urgent need for region-specific vaccine strategies, the integration of molecular diagnostics into routine screening, and targeted public health interventions to reduce HPV disease.

HPV 相关口咽癌预后分层的免疫评分验证:一项国际多中心研究。

Validation of Immunoscore for Prognostic Stratification in HPV-associated Oropharyngeal Cancer: An International Multicenter Study.
作者： Dac Hung Nguyen, Amine Majdi, Florence Marliot, Valentine Houtart, Amos Kirilovsky, Assia Hijazi, Tessa Fredriksen, Nicolas De Sousa Carvalho, Anne-Sophie Bach, Anne-Laure Gaultier
摘要中文翻译目的:优化 HPV 相关 OPSCC 中的 治疗 仍然具有挑战性,因为当前基于 TNM 和吸烟的风险分层不足以捕获肿瘤生物异质性,这凸显了对更多预后生物标志物的需求。我们报告了 HPV 相关 OPSCC 中免疫评分 (IS) 的首次验证。实验设计:我们分析了来自国际多中心 队列研究 (2015-2024) 的 191 例 HPV 相关(p16+ 和 HPV DNA/RNA+)OPSCC 患者,包括法国单中心回顾性训练 队列研究 (n = 48) 和三个验证 队列研究:法国单中心回顾性 (n = 48)、法国多中心前瞻性(n = 50) 和美国多中心回顾性研究 (n = 45)。IS 量化肿瘤核心和浸润边缘的 CD3+ 和 CD8+ 密度,其截止值在训练 队列研究 中定义,并在 队列研究 中进行验证。与无病生存期 (DFS)、总生存期 (OS) 和复发时间 (TTR) 的关联性以及 3'RNA-seq 和序贯免疫荧光分析进行了评估。结果:中位年龄 65 岁;80% 男性;74% 吸烟者;66% T1-2;82% N0-1。IS-High 患者在 队列研究 中具有优异的 3 年 DFS(所有对数秩 P < .05)。多变量分析将 IS-Low 确定为独立预后因素,对 DFS 调整后的影响大小最大(HR 9.03;95% CI:4.02-20.31;P < .001)。组合 IS 临床模型显示出比纯临床模型更好的 DFS 预测(C 指数 0.82 与 0.70;P < .0001)。TTR 和 OS 也观察到类似的结果。IS-High 肿瘤表现出丰富的淋巴和骨髓细胞群,与免疫较差的 IS-Low 肿瘤形成鲜明对比。结论:IS 在预后表现方面优于临床变量。通过识别生物学上不同的风险群体,IS 可以实现生物标志物引导、风险适应的 治疗 策略,包括 治疗 降级和强化。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在 HPV 相关口咽癌中优化 治疗 仍然具有挑战性,因为当前基于 TNM 和吸烟的风险分层无法捕获肿瘤的生物异质性,因此需要更好的预后生物标志物;本研究旨在验证免疫评分作为这样的生物标志物。
设计与对象
该研究是对 191 名 HPV 相关(p16+ 和 HPV DNA/RNA+)OPSCC 患者(2015-2024 年)进行的免疫评分国际多中心验证,包括一项法国单中心回顾性训练 队列研究 (n=48) 和三项验证 队列研究(法国回顾性 n=48、法国前瞻性 n=50、美国回顾性 n=45),其中 IS使用训练 队列研究 中定义的截止值通过肿瘤核心和侵袭性边缘中的 CD3+ 和 CD8+ 密度进行量化,并在 队列研究 中进行验证。
核心方法
通过流式细胞术测量免疫评分,评估肿瘤核心和浸润边缘的 CD3+ 和 CD8+ 细胞密度,并将无病生存期、总生存期和复发时间定义为主要结局;通过多变量 Cox 模型和 C 指数比较来评估关联性。
主要结果
中位患者年龄为65岁,其中80%为男性,74%为吸烟者,66%为T1‐2期,82%为N0‐1期;IS‐High患者表现出显著更高的3年无病生存率(log‐rank P < .05),且IS‐Low是无病生存期(DFS)的独立预后因素,其风险比(HR)为9.03(95%置信区间4.02‐20.31;P < .001);结合IS‐临床模型可改善DFS预测(C‐指数0.82 vs 0.70;P < .0001),且在复发时间及总生存期的预测中也观察到类似改善,而IS‐High肿瘤相较于免疫贫乏的IS‐Low肿瘤表现出更丰富的淋巴样和髓样细胞群体。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,Immunoscore 在预后表现方面优于临床变量,并确定了生物学上不同的风险组,这些风险组可能能够实现生物标志物引导、风险适应的 治疗 策略,这代表了免疫评分在 HPV 相关口咽癌中的首次验证。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,免疫评分可用于对患者进行风险适应治疗分层,有可能改善 HPV 相关口咽癌的临床决策和结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于来自有限地理区域的回顾性和前瞻性 队列研究 以及来自单一训练 队列研究 的截止值,再加上证据仅是抽象的事实,限制了置信度,但多中心设计和大样本量提供了中等的证据强度。

Validation of Immunoscore for Prognostic Stratification in HPV-associated Oropharyngeal Cancer: An International Multicenter Study.

Authors: Dac Hung Nguyen, Amine Majdi, Florence Marliot, Valentine Houtart, Amos Kirilovsky, Assia Hijazi, Tessa Fredriksen, Nicolas De Sousa Carvalho, Anne-Sophie Bach, Anne-Laure Gaultier
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: Optimizing treatment in HPV-associated OPSCC remains challenging, as current TNM- and smoking-based risk stratification inadequately captures tumor biological heterogeneity, highlighting the need for more prognostic biomarkers. We report the first validation of the Immunoscore (IS) in HPV-associated OPSCC. EXPERIMENTAL DESIGN: We analyzed 191 HPV-associated (p16+ and HPV DNA/RNA+) OPSCC patients from an international multicenter cohort (2015-2024), including a French monocentric retrospective training cohort (n = 48) and three validation cohorts: French monocentric retrospective (n = 48), French multicenter prospective (n = 50), and US multicenter retrospective (n = 45). IS quantified CD3+ and CD8+ densities in tumor cores and invasive margins, with cut-offs defined in the training cohort and validated across cohorts. Associations with disease-free survival (DFS), overall survival (OS), and time to recurrence (TTR) were assessed, alongside 3'RNA-seq and sequential immunofluorescence profiling. RESULTS: Median age 65; 80% male; 74% smokers; 66% T1-2; 82% N0-1. IS-High patients had superior 3-year DFS across cohorts (all log-rank P < .05). Multivariable analysis identified IS-Low as the independent prognostic factor with the largest adjusted effect size for DFS (HR 9.03; 95% CI: 4.02-20.31; P < .001). The combined IS-clinical model showed better DFS prediction than the clinical-only model (C-index 0.82 vs 0.70; P < .0001). Similar results were observed for TTR and OS. IS-High tumors showed enriched lymphoid and myeloid populations, contrasting with immune-poor IS-Low tumors. CONCLUSIONS: IS outperformed clinical variables in prognostic performance. By identifying biologically distinct risk groups, IS may enable biomarker-guided, risk-adapted treatment strategies, including treatment de-escalation and intensification.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Optimizing treatment in HPV‐associated oropharyngeal cancer remains challenging because current TNM‐ and smoking‐based risk stratification fails to capture tumor biological heterogeneity, creating a need for better prognostic biomarkers; this study aimed to validate the Immunoscore as such a biomarker.
Design and population
The study was an international multicenter validation of Immunoscore in 191 HPV‐associated (p16+ and HPV DNA/RNA+) OPSCC patients (2015‐2024), comprising a French monocentric retrospective training cohort (n=48) and three validation cohorts (French retrospective n=48, French prospective n=50, US retrospective n=45), with IS quantified by CD3+ and CD8+ densities in tumor cores and invasive margins using cut‐offs defined in the training cohort and validated across cohorts.
Core methods
Immunoscore was measured by flow cytometry assessing CD3+ and CD8+ cell densities in tumor cores and invasive margins, and disease‐free survival, overall survival and time to recurrence were defined as primary outcomes; associations were evaluated with multivariable Cox models and C‐index comparison.
Main results
Median patient age was 65 with 80% male, 74% smokers, 66% T1‐2 and 82% N0‐1; IS‐High patients exhibited significantly higher 3‐year disease‐free survival (log‐rank P < .05), and IS‐Low was an independent prognostic factor for DFS with HR 9.03 (95% CI 4.02‐20.31; P < .001); the combined IS‐clinical model improved DFS prediction (C‐index 0.82 vs 0.70; P < .0001) and similar gains were observed for time to recurrence and overall survival, while IS‐High tumors showed enriched lymphoid and myeloid populations compared with immune‐poor IS‐Low tumors.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that Immunoscore outperformed clinical variables in prognostic performance and identified biologically distinct risk groups that may enable biomarker‐guided, risk‐adapted treatment strategies, representing the first validation of Immunoscore in HPV‐associated oropharyngeal cancer.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that Immunoscore can be used to stratify patients for risk‐adapted therapy, potentially improving clinical decision‐making and outcomes in HPV‐associated oropharyngeal cancer.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s reliance on retrospective and prospective cohorts from limited geographic regions and cut‐offs derived from a single training cohort, together with the fact that the evidence is abstract‐only, limits confidence, but the multicenter design and large sample size provide moderate evidence strength.

日本关于 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 的决策过程和支持需求:对母亲的定性访谈研究

Decision-making process and support needs regarding the human papillomavirus vaccine in Japan: a qualitative interview study with mothers
作者： Tomomi Funaki, Izumi Kusuki
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在阐明女儿符合 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 条件的母亲的决策过程和支持需求。我们对 15 名日本母亲进行了半结构化访谈,她们代表了 17 名 12 至 20 岁的符合条件的女儿。进行了主题分析以进行数据分析。我们提取了41个概念代码和15个类别,并将其分为四个分析主题:“决策的促进者”、“决策的抑制者”、“决策过程的波动”和“母亲需要的支持”。妈妈们在做决定的过程中经历着情绪的波动和冲突,反复感到焦虑和安心,在权衡自己的期望和担忧的同时试图做出自己认为满意的决定。他们的信息和支持需求在整个决策过程中各不相同。母亲们在 HPV 疫苗接种 决策过程中经历了情绪波动和冲突,她们的信息和支持需求根据她们个人的担忧和做出决定的准备程度而有所不同。因此,决策支持应该在整个决策过程中回应每位母亲的关切和需求。跨专业合作对于为母亲提供适当的信息和支持也很重要。这些发现可能有助于为日本的 HPV 疫苗接种 决策制定量身定制的支持。

问题与背景
本研究旨在阐明女儿符合 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 条件的母亲的决策过程和支持需求。

进行了主题分析以进行数据分析。

Decision-making process and support needs regarding the human papillomavirus vaccine in Japan: a qualitative interview study with mothers

Authors: Tomomi Funaki, Izumi Kusuki
Original abstract (English)
This study aimed to clarify the decision-making process and support needs of mothers with daughters eligible for human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination. We conducted semi-structured interviews with 15 Japanese mothers, representing 17 eligible daughters aged 12–20 years. Thematic analysis was performed for data analysis. We extracted 41 concept codes and 15 categories and classified them into four analytical themes: “facilitators of decision-making,” “inhibitors of decision-making,” “fluctuations in the decision-making process,” and “support that mothers need.” Mothers experienced emotional fluctuations and conflicts throughout the decision-making process, repeatedly feeling anxious and reassured and weighing their expectations against their concerns while trying to make a decision they considered satisfactory. Their information and support needs varied across the decision-making process. Mothers experienced emotional fluctuations and conflicts throughout the HPV vaccination decision-making process, and their information and support needs varied according to their individual concerns and readiness to make a decision. Therefore, decision-making support should be responsive to each mother’s concerns and needs throughout the decision-making process. Interprofessional collaboration may also be important in providing mothers with appropriate information and support. These findings may contribute to the development of tailored support for HPV vaccination decision-making in Japan.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

人乳头瘤病毒 中的后向分岔和 Hopf 分岔——具有不完善的 疫苗接种 和免疫力减弱的癌症模型

Backward and Hopf bifurcations in a human papillomavirus–cancer model with imperfect vaccination and waning immunity
作者： Din Prathumwan, Kingkan Sunthonphong, Sirawit Phakmee, Inthira Chaiya, Kamonchat Trachoo
摘要中文翻译简介 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是最常见的性传播感染,也是几乎所有 宫颈癌 的原因,但真正的 疫苗接种 计划的两个特征使消除变得复杂:可用的 疫苗 不完善,使接种疫苗的个体部分易受影响,并且自然获得的免疫力减弱。我们询问当两种机制都存在时,将基本再生数降低到单位以下是否足以消除。方法 我们制定并分析了六室 (SEIRVC) 确定性模型,该模型结合了不完善的 疫苗 和免疫力下降以及明确进展为 HPV 相关癌症类别的情况。我们建立正性、有界性和不变区域;通过下一代矩阵法推导R 0;使用 Routh-Hurwitz 准则、Lyapunov 函数以及 Li 和 Muldowney 的几何方法来表征平衡及其稳定性;并在灵敏度指数和仿真的支持下分析分叉结构。结果繁殖数可加性地分解为易感路线和疫苗突破路线,并且当R 0 < 1时,无病平衡精确地局部渐近稳定,并且在显式条件下全局如此。将地方性平衡减少到二次方根表明,泄漏的 疫苗 与免疫力减弱一起产生向后分叉,因此 R 0 < 1 是必要的,但不足以消除和控制,必须清除转折点 R 0 c 1。高于阈值,独特的地方性平衡可能会通过 Hopf 分叉失去稳定性,从而产生持续的振荡,其幅度在癌症类别中被放大,在恒定参数下的一个周期内变化为 10 2 数量级。这两种现象都需要两种机制,并在 疫苗 变得完全保护或永久免疫时消失,这是证明地方性平衡的全局稳定性的极限;双参数连续表明这两种制度是不相交的替代方案。讨论 敏感性分析给出 sgnY β = sgn(θ−λ H ),因此扩大保护性不足的 疫苗 会提高 R 0,而衰减率的弹性为零,但决定了两种非经典状态。因此,阈值数量本身并不是 HPV 控制的可靠指南,并且 监测 必须随着时间的推移跟踪 监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
简介 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是最常见的性传播感染,也是几乎所有 宫颈癌 的原因,但真正的 疫苗接种 计划的两个特征使消除变得复杂:可用的 疫苗 不完善,使接种疫苗的个体部分易受影响,并且自然获得的免疫力减弱。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
方法 我们制定并分析了六室 (SEIRVC) 确定性模型,该模型结合了不完善的 疫苗 和免疫力下降以及明确进展为 HPV 相关癌症类别的情况。
主要结果
结果繁殖数可加性地分解为易感路线和疫苗突破路线,并且当R 0 < 1时,无病平衡精确地局部渐近稳定,并且在显式条件下全局如此。
解释与创新
讨论 敏感性分析给出 sgnY β = sgn(θ−λ H ),因此扩大保护性不足的 疫苗 会提高 R 0,而衰减率的弹性为零,但决定了两种非经典状态。
科研与公卫意义
因此,阈值数量本身并不是 HPV 控制的可靠指南,并且 监测 必须随着时间的推移跟踪 监测。
局限与证据强度
这两种现象都需要两种机制,并在 疫苗 变得完全保护或永久免疫时消失,这是证明地方性平衡的全局稳定性的极限;双参数连续表明这两种制度是不相交的替代方案。

Backward and Hopf bifurcations in a human papillomavirus–cancer model with imperfect vaccination and waning immunity

Authors: Din Prathumwan, Kingkan Sunthonphong, Sirawit Phakmee, Inthira Chaiya, Kamonchat Trachoo
Original abstract (English)
Introduction Human papillomavirus (HPV) is the most common sexually transmitted infection and the cause of virtually all cervical cancer, yet elimination is complicated by two features of real vaccination programmes: available vaccines are imperfect, leaving vaccinated individuals partially susceptible, and naturally acquired immunity wanes. We ask whether reducing the basic reproduction number below unity is sufficient for elimination when both mechanisms are present. Methods We formulate and analyse a six-compartment ( SEIRVC ) deterministic model combining an imperfect vaccine and waning immunity with explicit progression to an HPV-related cancer class. We establish positivity, boundedness, and an invariant region; derive R 0 by the next-generation matrix method; characterize the equilibria and their stability using Routh–Hurwitz criteria, Lyapunov functions, and the geometric approach of Li and Muldowney; and analyse the bifurcation structure, supported by sensitivity indices and simulation. Results The reproduction number decomposes additively into a susceptible and a vaccine-breakthrough route, and the disease-free equilibrium is locally asymptotically stable precisely when R 0 < 1, and globally so under an explicit condition. Reducing the endemic equilibria to the roots of a quadratic shows that a leaky vaccine together with waning immunity produces a backward bifurcation, so that R 0 < 1 is necessary but not sufficient for elimination and control must clear a turning point R 0 c 1 . Above threshold the unique endemic equilibrium can lose stability through a Hopf bifurcation, giving sustained oscillations whose amplitude is magnified in the cancer class, which varies by a factor of order 10 2 within a cycle under constant parameters. Both phenomena require both mechanisms and vanish when the vaccine becomes perfectly protective or immunity permanent, the limit in which global stability of the endemic equilibrium is proved; two-parameter continuation shows the two regimes to be disjoint alternatives. Discussion Sensitivity analysis gives sgnΥ β = sgn(θ−λ H ) so scaling up an insufficiently protective vaccine raises R 0 , while the waning rate has zero elasticity yet determines both non-classical regimes. Threshold quantities alone are therefore an unreliable guide to HPV control, and surveillance must track prevalence over time.
View research seven-element analysis

丹麦 流行病学 的变化和 HPV 相关口咽癌的生存率:四十年的人口数据

Changing epidemiology and survival of HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer in Denmark: four decades of population-based data
作者： Kathrine Kronberg Jakobsen, Jakob Myllerup Jensen, Joachim Hansen, Christian von Buchwald, Christian Grønhøj
摘要中文翻译背景 口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 经历了巨大的流行病学和临床变化,人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关肿瘤的比例不断增加,烟草接触量下降。我们调查了丹麦 OPSCC 患者的患者特征、HPV/p16 状态、治疗 模式和生存率的时间变化。方法 从哥本哈根口咽癌数据库(2000-2023 年)和丹麦国家患者癌症登记处(DCR;1980-2021 年)中确定 OPSCC 患者。哥本哈根数据库提供了详细的临床和病理数据,包括 HPV/p16 状态,而 DCR 可以评估长期生存趋势。在诊断期间评估时间变化。估计总生存期、相对生存期和限制平均生存时间。结果 在哥本哈根数据库中的 3,631 名 OPSCC 患者中,诊断 的中位年龄从 2000-2005 年的 59 岁增加到 2018-2023 年的 64 岁(p<0.001)。HPV+/p16+患者的比例从2000-2005年的43.5%显着增加到2018-2023年的62.8%。从不吸烟的比例也从 0.5% 增加到 23.5%,而≥20包年的比例从 73.2% 下降到 52.1% (p<0.001)。治疗 模式发生了很大变化,单纯放疗从 87.6% 下降到 27.9%,放化疗从 1.6% 增加到 46.3%。在 DCR 的 9,379 名患者中,五年相对生存率从 1980-1984 年的 31.8% 增加到 2015-2019 年的 70.1%。在HPV+/p16+患者中,五年总生存率从2000-2005年的63.1%增加到2018-2023年的83.7%。结论 在丹麦,OPSCC 经历了重大的流行病学和治疗变化,同时生存率显着提高,包括 HPV+/p16+ 患者。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查丹麦 OPSCC 患者特征、HPV/p16 状态、治疗 模式和生存率的时间变化,解决不断变化的 流行病学 和 HPV 相关口咽癌的临床结果。
设计与对象
该研究纳入了来自哥本哈根口咽癌数据库(2000-2023)和丹麦国家患者癌症登记处(DCR;1980-2021)的 OPSCC 患者。
核心方法
从哥本哈根数据库中确定患者的详细临床和病理数据,包括 HPV/p16 状态,并从 DCR 中确定长期生存趋势。
主要结果
在哥本哈根数据库中的 3,631 名 OPSCC 患者中,诊断 的中位年龄从 2000-2005 年的 59 岁增加到 2018-2023 年的 64 岁。HPV+/p16+患者的比例从2000-2005年的43.5%显着增加到2018-2023年的62.8%。从不吸烟的比例也从 0.5% 增加到 23.5%,而≥20包年的比例从 73.2% 下降到 52.1%。治疗 模式发生了很大变化,单纯放疗从 87.6% 下降到 27.9%,放化疗从 1.6% 增加到 46.3%。在 DCR 的 9,379 名患者中,五年相对生存率从 1980-1984 年的 31.8% 增加到 2015-2019 年的 70.1%。在HPV+/p16+患者中,五年总生存率从2000-2005年的63.1%增加到2018-2023年的83.7%。
解释与创新
研究发现 OPSCC 流行病学 和 治疗 模式发生了显着变化,生存率显着提高,特别是在 HPV+/p16+ 患者中。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,患者特征的变化和 治疗 方法有助于改善丹麦 HPV 相关口咽癌的生存结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到对登记数据的依赖和潜在的选择偏差的限制。由于该研究的观察性质,证据强度中等。

Changing epidemiology and survival of HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer in Denmark: four decades of population-based data

Authors: Kathrine Kronberg Jakobsen, Jakob Myllerup Jensen, Joachim Hansen, Christian von Buchwald, Christian Grønhøj
Original abstract (English)
Background Oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) has undergone substantial epidemiological and clinical changes, with an increasing proportion of human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated tumours and declining tobacco exposure. We investigated temporal changes in patient characteristics, HPV/p16 status, treatment patterns, and survival among patients with OPSCC in Denmark. Methods Patients with OPSCC were identified from the Copenhagen Oropharyngeal Cancer Database (2000–2023) and the Danish National Patient Cancer Registry (DCR; 1980–2021). The Copenhagen database provided detailed clinical and pathological data, including HPV/p16 status, while the DCR enabled assessment of long-term survival trends. Temporal changes were evaluated across diagnostic periods. Overall survival, relative survival, and restricted mean survival time were estimated. Results Among 3,631 OPSCC patients in the Copenhagen database, median age at diagnosis increased from 59 years in 2000–2005 to 64 years in 2018–2023 (p<0.001). The proportion of HPV+/p16+ patients increased significantly from 43.5% in 2000–2005 to 62.8% in 2018-2023. The proportion of never smokers also increased from 0.5% to 23.5%, while ≥20 pack-years decreased from 73.2% to 52.1% (p<0.001). Treatment patterns changed substantially, with radiotherapy alone decreasing from 87.6% to 27.9% and chemoradiotherapy increasing from 1.6% to 46.3%. Among 9,379 patients in the DCR, five-year relative survival increased from 31.8% in 1980–1984 to 70.1% in 2015-2019. Among HPV+/p16+ patients, five-year overall survival increased from 63.1% in 2000–2005 to 83.7% in 2018–2023. Conclusion In Denmark, OPSCC has undergone substantial epidemiological and therapeutic changes accompanied by marked improvements in survival, including among HPV+/p16+ patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate temporal changes in patient characteristics, HPV/p16 status, treatment patterns, and survival among patients with OPSCC in Denmark, addressing the changing epidemiology and clinical outcomes of HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer.
Design and population
The study included patients with OPSCC from the Copenhagen Oropharyngeal Cancer Database (2000–2023) and the Danish National Patient Cancer Registry (DCR; 1980–2021).
Core methods
Patients were identified from the Copenhagen database for detailed clinical and pathological data, including HPV/p16 status, and from the DCR for long-term survival trends.
Main results
Among 3,631 OPSCC patients in the Copenhagen database, median age at diagnosis increased from 59 years in 2000–2005 to 64 years in 2018–2023. The proportion of HPV+/p16+ patients increased significantly from 43.5% in 2000–2005 to 62.8% in 2018-2023. The proportion of never smokers also increased from 0.5% to 23.5%, while ≥20 pack-years decreased from 73.2% to 52.1%. Treatment patterns changed substantially, with radiotherapy alone decreasing from 87.6% to 27.9% and chemoradiotherapy increasing from 1.6% to 46.3%. Among 9,379 patients in the DCR, five-year relative survival increased from 31.8% in 1980–1984 to 70.1% in 2015-2019. Among HPV+/p16+ patients, five-year overall survival increased from 63.1% in 2000–2005 to 83.7% in 2018–2023.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found substantial changes in OPSCC epidemiology and treatment patterns, with marked improvements in survival, particularly among HPV+/p16+ patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that changes in patient characteristics and treatment approaches have contributed to improved survival outcomes for HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer in Denmark.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the reliance on registry data and the potential for selection bias. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

Cutavirus DNA 在 人乳头瘤病毒 DNA 阴性口咽鳞状细胞癌中的预后相关性:一项回顾性 队列研究 研究

Prognostic relevance of cutavirus DNA in human papillomavirus DNA-negative oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma: a retrospective cohort study
作者： Yumiko Hashida, Shuichi Matsumoto, Masanori Teshima, Masanori Daibata
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 状态是口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 最强的预后决定因素。由于 HPV 阳性和 HPV 阴性 OPSCC 代表不同的疾病实体,因此这些亚组之间的 患病率 和其他病毒感染的预后相关性可能有所不同。本研究旨在根据 HPV DNA 状态确定 OPSCC 中肿瘤相关病毒的 患病率,并评估其与临床结果的关联,特别是 HPV DNA 阴性疾病。方法 使用实时聚合酶链反应分析 106 名日本 OPSCC 患者的肿瘤标本中是否存在 HPV、皮肤病毒 (CuV)、EB 病毒 (EBV)、人类巨细胞病毒 (HCMV) 和默克尔细胞多瘤病毒 (MCPyV)。根据 HPV DNA 状态评估病毒 患病率 和载量,并评估病毒检测与临床病理特征之间的关联。使用 Kaplan-Meier 方法和 Cox 比例风险回归模型分析无进展生存期 (PFS) 和总生存期 (OS)。结果 在总体 队列研究 中,分别在 47 例(44%)、23 例(22%)、21 例(20%)、6 例(6%)和 1 例(1%)病例中检测到 HPV、CuV、EBV、HCMV 和 MCPyV DNA。在 HPV DNA 阴性 OPSCC 病例中 ( n = 59),分别在 14 例 (24%)、8 例 (14%)、3 例 (5%) 和 1 例 (2%) 例中检测到 CuV、EBV、HCMV 和 MCPyV DNA。根据 HPV DNA 状态,病毒载量没有显着差异。在 HPV DNA 阴性亚组中,CuV DNA 阳性与较短的 PFS 显着相关(对数秩 P < 0.001),而与 OS 没有显着相关。单变量 Cox 回归分析确定 CuV DNA 阳性是与 HPV DNA 阴性 OPSCC 中 PFS 显着相关的唯一病毒因素(风险比,3.14;95% 置信区间,1.55–6.35;P = 0.001)。在调整临床病理因素后,CuV DNA 阳性仍然与较短的 PFS 显着相关,而 EBV、HCMV 和 MCPyV DNA 阳性与临床结果没有显着相关。结论 CuV DNA 阳性与 HPV DNA 阴性 OPSCC 中较短的 PFS 相关。CuV DNA 阳性可能与 HPV DNA 阴性 OPSCC 具有预后相关性,尽管其生物学意义和因果相关性仍不清楚。需要进行更大规模的研究来验证这些发现并进一步阐明 CuV DNA 检测在 OPSCC 中的重要性。

核心方法

主要结果

局限与证据强度
需要进行更大规模的研究来验证这些发现并进一步阐明 CuV DNA 检测在 OPSCC 中的重要性。

Prognostic relevance of cutavirus DNA in human papillomavirus DNA-negative oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma: a retrospective cohort study

Authors: Yumiko Hashida, Shuichi Matsumoto, Masanori Teshima, Masanori Daibata
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) status is the strongest prognostic determinant in oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC). As HPV-positive and HPV-negative OPSCC represent distinct disease entities, the prevalence and prognostic relevance of other viral infections may differ between these subgroups. This study aimed to determine the prevalence of tumor-associated viruses in OPSCC according to HPV DNA status and evaluate their association with clinical outcomes, with particular emphasis on HPV DNA-negative disease. Methods Tumor specimens from 106 Japanese patients with OPSCC were analyzed for the presence of HPV, cutavirus (CuV), Epstein–Barr virus (EBV), human cytomegalovirus (HCMV), and Merkel cell polyomavirus (MCPyV) using real-time polymerase chain reaction. Viral prevalence and loads were assessed according to HPV DNA status, and associations between viral detection and clinicopathological characteristics were evaluated. Progression-free survival (PFS) and overall survival (OS) were analyzed using Kaplan–Meier methods and Cox proportional hazards regression models. Results In the overall cohort, HPV, CuV, EBV, HCMV, and MCPyV DNA was detected in 47 (44%), 23 (22%), 21 (20%), 6 (6%), and 1 (1%) cases, respectively. Among HPV DNA-negative OPSCC cases ( n = 59), CuV, EBV, HCMV, and MCPyV DNA was detected in 14 (24%), 8 (14%), 3 (5%), and 1 (2%) cases, respectively. Viral loads did not differ significantly according to HPV DNA status. Within the HPV DNA-negative subgroup, CuV DNA positivity was significantly associated with shorter PFS (log-rank P < 0.001), whereas no significant association was observed with OS. Univariable Cox regression analysis identified CuV DNA positivity as the only viral factor significantly associated with PFS in HPV DNA-negative OPSCC (hazard ratio, 3.14; 95% confidence interval, 1.55–6.35; P = 0.001). CuV DNA positivity remained significantly associated with shorter PFS after adjustment for clinicopathological factors, whereas EBV, HCMV, and MCPyV DNA positivity was not significantly associated with clinical outcomes. Conclusions CuV DNA positivity was associated with shorter PFS in HPV DNA-negative OPSCC. CuV DNA positivity may have prognostic relevance in HPV DNA-negative OPSCC, although its biological significance and causal relevance remain unclear. Larger studies are warranted to validate these findings and further clarify the significance of CuV DNA detection in OPSCC.
