口腔上皮发育不良、组织病理学 诊断 和 人乳头瘤病毒 感染的作用

Oral epithelial dysplasia, role of histopathological diagnosis and human papillomavirus infection
作者： Riikka Ellonen
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
口腔上皮发育不良(OED)被认为是口腔鳞状细胞癌(OSCC)的主要组织病理学前体。不典型增生的组织病理学分级仍然是 OED 评估的基石,但其预测价值有限。
设计与对象
从口腔病理登记处回顾性检索 1982 年至 2011 年间来自芬兰图尔库大学牙科研究所口腔病理学和放射学系的所有 OED 患者。患有 OSCC 的患者是通过芬兰癌症登记处的芬兰社会安全号码来识别的。
核心方法
为了研究 HPV 检测在 OED 中的作用及其进展,我们在蛋白质水平 (p16)、HPV DNA 水平(聚合酶链式反应,PCR)和 高危型HPV mRNA 的存在(原位杂交,ISH)上检测了 HPV。三种 HPV 实验室检测方法(p16 p=0.2、PCR p=0.7 和 mRNA ISH p=0.7)单独或组合均与恶性转化无关。
主要结果
OED 病变最常见的部位是活动舌头。OED 案例中,轻度占 63.7%,中度占 29.5%,重度占 3.4%,其余 OED 未分级。重新评估为两级分级后,84.1%为低度不典型增生,15.9%为高度不典型增生。OED 的平均随访时间为 5.9 年(范围 0.7-29.0,SD=5.9)。在 OED 患者中,7.8% 进展为 OSCC。
解释与创新
With statistical analysis, only location on the mobile tongue (Fisher’s exact test, p=0.016) and the severity of OED were associated with the risk of OSCC development (Hazard ratio, HR=3.44, 95% confidence interval, CI 0.83-14.19). The more severe grade of OED had the shorter time for OSCC development (Jonkcheere Terpstra -test p=0.025).
科研与公卫意义
只有两个 OED 因素具有恶变的预测价值:OED 在活动舌上的位置和更严重的 OED 组织病理学分级。我们的研究结果支持 OED 的致癌作用尚不确定的观点。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据没有报告对照组的使用。所提供的证据并未报告前瞻性研究设计的使用。证据强度中等。

Oral epithelial dysplasia, role of histopathological diagnosis and human papillomavirus infection

Authors: Riikka Ellonen
Original abstract (English)
Oral epithelial dysplasia (OED) is regarded as the main histopathological precursor to oral squamous cell carcinoma (OSCC). Histopathological grading of dysplasia remains the cornerstone of OED assessment and yet, its predictive value is limited. In addition to histopathological diagnosis of OED, other histopathological diagnosis can simultaneously occur in a lesion. The World Health Organization (WHO) recognized another distinct entity of OED year 2017: human papillomavirus (HPV) associated OED. The aim of this dissertation study was to evaluate three factors in OED that could influence the malignant transformation: classification of OED, additional histopathological diagnoses to OED and HPV infection. All OED patients from the Department of Oral Pathology and Radiology, Institute of Dentistry, University of Turku, Turku, Finland between the years 1982 and 2011 were retrospectively retrieved from the oral pathology registry. Patients who developed OSCC were identified from the Finnish Cancer Registry by their Finnish social security number. ICD codes C00 and C02 – C06 were considered as OSCC. First, three-tier grading was used representing the real-life situation. Then the effect of additional histopathological diagnosis to malignant transformation was evaluated. Later, re-evaluation of OEDs with two-tier grading was performed to investigate if it has more objectivity and therefore could better predict OSCC development. To investigate the role of HPV detection in OED and its progress, HPV was detected on protein level (p16), HPV DNA level (polymerase chain reaction, PCR) and the presence of high-risk HPV mRNA (in situ hybridization, ISH). There were 922 patients with OED in the oral pathology registry. Their mean age was 60 years and 69% were women. The most common location for OED lesion was mobile tongue. There were found 63.7% mild, 29.5% moderate and severe 3.4% of the OEDs cases, and the rest OEDs were not graded. After the re-evaluation into two-tier grading, 84.1% were low-grade dysplasia and 15.9% high-grade dysplasia. The mean follow-up time of OEDs was 5.9 years (range 0.7-29.0, SD=5.9). Of OED patients, 7.8% progressed to OSCC. Of the additional diagnoses, hyperkeratosis, hyperplasia, and ulcer were the most common findings, found in 44.9%, 27.3% and 18.1% of the samples, respectively. Positivity for HPV with the three laboratory methods varied between 0.9-5.9%. With statistical analysis, only location on the mobile tongue (Fisher’s exact test, p=0.016) and the severity of OED were associated with the risk of OSCC development (Hazard ratio, HR=3.44, 95% confidence interval, CI 0.83-14.19). The more severe grade of OED had the shorter time for OSCC development (Jonkcheere Terpstra -test p=0.025). Malignant transformation was not associated with any of the three laboratory detection methods of HPV (p16 p=0.2, PCR p=0.7, and mRNA ISH p=0.7) individually or combined. Only two factors with OED had predictive value of malignant transformation: location of OED on the mobile tongue and the more severe histopathological grade of OED. Our findings support the concept that the carcinogenesis of OED is uncertain. HPV-infection was unreliably detected and did not change the risk for malignant transformation. Alternative biomarkers than histopathological picture and HPV detection are needed for the OED risk assessment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Oral epithelial dysplasia (OED) is regarded as the main histopathological precursor to oral squamous cell carcinoma (OSCC). Histopathological grading of dysplasia remains the cornerstone of OED assessment and yet, its predictive value is limited.
Design and population
All OED patients from the Department of Oral Pathology and Radiology, Institute of Dentistry, University of Turku, Turku, Finland between the years 1982 and 2011 were retrospectively retrieved from the oral pathology registry. Patients who developed OSCC were identified from the Finnish Cancer Registry by their Finnish social security number.
Core methods
To investigate the role of HPV detection in OED and its progress, HPV was detected on protein level (p16), HPV DNA level (polymerase chain reaction, PCR) and the presence of high-risk HPV mRNA (in situ hybridization, ISH). Malignant transformation was not associated with any of the three laboratory detection methods of HPV (p16 p=0.2, PCR p=0.7, and mRNA ISH p=0.7) individually or combined.
Main results
The most common location for OED lesion was mobile tongue. There were found 63.7% mild, 29.5% moderate and severe 3.4% of the OEDs cases, and the rest OEDs were not graded. After the re-evaluation into two-tier grading, 84.1% were low-grade dysplasia and 15.9% high-grade dysplasia. The mean follow-up time of OEDs was 5.9 years (range 0.7-29.0, SD=5.9). Of OED patients, 7.8% progressed to OSCC.

With statistical analysis, only location on the mobile tongue (Fisher’s exact test, p=0.016) and the severity of OED were associated with the risk of OSCC development (Hazard ratio, HR=3.44, 95% confidence interval, CI 0.83-14.19). The more severe grade of OED had the shorter time for OSCC development (Jonkcheere Terpstra -test p=0.025).
Scientific and public-health significance
Only two factors with OED had predictive value of malignant transformation: location of OED on the mobile tongue and the more severe histopathological grade of OED. Our findings support the concept that the carcinogenesis of OED is uncertain.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report the use of a control group. The supplied evidence does not report the use of a prospective study design. The evidence strength is moderate.

HPV 疫苗接种 经济评估中的方法驱动因素、报告完整性和可转移性:范围界定审查

Methodological drivers, reporting completeness, and transferability in HPV vaccination economic evaluations: A scoping review
作者： Adnan Kisa, Sezer Kisa, Adnan Kısa, Sezer Kısa
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 被广泛报道为具有成本效益,但报告的增量成本效益比 (ICER) 是由模型结构、假设、比较器选择、疫苗 价格、阈值选择和本地筛选环境决定的。之前的评论总结了成本效益的研究结果,但很少研究为什么结论在不同环境下会有所不同。目的:绘制 HPV 进化 经济评估中的方法论 进化、变异驱动因素、报告完整性、方法论可信度和可转移性。方法:使用 Arksey 和 O'Malley 提出的方法框架进行范围界定审查,并由 Levac 等人完善,并参考 JBI 范围界定指南。报告遵循 PRISMA-ScR,并且该协议已在开放科学框架 (OSF) 上预先注册。PubMed、Scopus、PsycINFO、Embase、Web of Science 和 CINAHL 的检索时间为 2003 年 1 月 1 日至 2025 年 12 月 31 日,最终检索于 2026 年 1 月 2 日进行。映射了 44 条记录。三十三个完整的比较经济评价形成了初步综合;11 条部分或非标准经济证据记录被保留作为背景证据。回顾性地应用了 2022 年综合卫生经济评估报告标准 (CHEERS 2022) 清单来评估报告的完整性,并使用描述性结构化评估来检查六个卫生经济领域的方法学可信度。使用 Welte 知情评估来检查目标情境情境化。结果:证据基础从早期静态 队列研究 模型发展到动态 传播 和基于个体的方法,而静态模型在以中低收入国家为重点的标准化工具分析中仍然很常见。变化的主要驱动因素是 疫苗 价格、折扣、群体免疫假设、交叉保护假设和阈值选择。报告的完整性各不相同,并不一定符合方法论的可信度。与研究自身决策制定的相关性并不意味着其 ICER 可以转移到其他国家。十一项背景记录显示了围绕交付成本、财政后果、吸收、风险收益框架和非基于 ICER 的决策支持的新政策问题。结论:HPV 疫苗接种 报告的经济价值取决于每次评估中使用的假设、比较器、疫苗 价格、阈值和当地筛查环境,而不是仅取决于 疫苗。未来的评估应明确证明比较器、模型结构、疫苗价格假设、贴现率、阈值、不确定性分析和可转移性假设的选择的合理性。尽管单剂 HPV 疫苗接种 的正式成本效益分析已经开始出现,但证据仍然有限,特别是在耐久性、衰退、交付可行性、中低收入国家中性别中立的 疫苗接种 以及代表性不足地区的具体国家评估方面。

Methodological drivers, reporting completeness, and transferability in HPV vaccination economic evaluations: A scoping review

Authors: Adnan Kisa, Sezer Kisa, Adnan Kısa, Sezer Kısa
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination is widely reported as cost-effective, but reported incremental cost-effectiveness ratios (ICERs) are shaped by model structure, assumptions, comparator choice, vaccine price, threshold selection, and local screening context. Previous reviews have summarized cost-effectiveness findings but have less often examined why conclusions differ across settings. OBJECTIVE: To map methodological evolution, drivers of variation, reporting completeness, methodological credibility, and transferability in HPV vaccination economic evaluations. METHODS: A scoping review was conducted using the methodological framework proposed by Arksey and O'Malley, refined by Levac et al., and informed by JBI guidance for scoping reviews. Reporting followed PRISMA-ScR, and the protocol was preregistered on the Open Science Framework (OSF). PubMed, Scopus, PsycINFO, Embase, Web of Science, and CINAHL were searched from January 1, 2003, to December 31, 2025, with a final search conducted on January 2, 2026. Forty-four records were mapped. Thirty-three full comparative economic evaluations formed the primary synthesis; 11 partial or non-standard economic evidence records were retained as contextual evidence. The Consolidated Health Economic Evaluation Reporting Standards 2022 (CHEERS 2022) checklist was applied retrospectively to assess reporting completeness, and a descriptive structured appraisal was used to examine methodological credibility across six health-economic domains. A Welte-informed assessment was used to examine target-context contextualization. RESULTS: The evidence base evolved from early static cohort models to dynamic transmission and individual-based approaches, while static models remained common in LMIC-focused standardized-tool analyses. The main drivers of variation were vaccine price, discounting, herd-immunity assumptions, cross-protection assumptions, and threshold selection. Reporting completeness varied and did not necessarily correspond to methodological credibility. Relevance to a study's own decision setting did not imply that its ICER could be transferred to other countries. Eleven contextual records showed emerging policy questions around delivery costs, fiscal consequences, uptake, risk-benefit framing, and non-ICER-based decision support. CONCLUSION: The economic value reported for HPV vaccination depends on the assumptions, comparators, vaccine prices, thresholds, and local screening contexts used in each evaluation rather than on the vaccine alone. Future evaluations should explicitly justify the choice of comparator, model structure, vaccine-price assumptions, discount rates, thresholds, uncertainty analysis, and transferability assumptions. Although formal cost-effectiveness analyses of single-dose HPV vaccination have begun to emerge, evidence remains limited, particularly regarding durability, waning, delivery feasibility, gender-neutral vaccination in LMICs, and country-specific evaluations in underrepresented regions.

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

社交媒体和 HPV 疫苗 犹豫不决:错误信息如何影响拉丁裔父母

Social Media and HPV Vaccine Hesitancy: How Misinformation Affects Latino Parents
作者： Abraham Aragones, Denise Bruno, Ilona Fridman, Isabella Sarria, Rachel Loia, Claudia Pallasa
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 在美国的覆盖率仍然不理想,拉丁裔社区面临着 HPV 相关癌症的不成比例的较低发病率和较高负担。社交媒体上的健康错误信息和虚假信息 (M&D) 是 HPV 疫苗 吸收的障碍,但很少有经验证据表明此类内容如何影响 疫苗 决策。我们对 98 名拉丁裔父母进行了一项横断面研究,这些父母来自两项随机、基于社区的前瞻性试验,这些试验在四名符合条件的美国参与者中进行,证实他们符合 HPV 疫苗 条件的孩子仍未接种疫苗,并报告了活跃的社交媒体使用情况。一项结构化调查评估了平台使用情况、访问频率、健康信息寻求行为以及 HPV 疫苗 特定社交媒体内容的暴露情况。所有 98 名家长均表示经常使用社交媒体。Facebook (68%)、Instagram (61%)、WhatsApp (61%) 和 YouTube (48%) 是最常用的平台。四分之三的家长 (77%,n = 75) 报告称,去年曾在社交媒体上曝光过与 疫苗 相关的内容,其中 99% (n = 74) 将其描述为负面。超过一半 (52%,n = 51) 的人回忆起 HPV 疫苗 的特定内容,所有这些人都将其描述为阴性。41% 的家长 (n = 40) 表示,感知到的负面社交媒体内容直接影响了他们推迟或拒绝 HPV 疫苗接种 的决定;在 HPV 特异性阴性暴露人群中,这一比例上升至 78%。据自我报告,社交媒体 M&D 是拉丁裔父母对 HPV 疫苗 犹豫不决的重要驱动因素。所遇到的 疫苗 内容的被动曝光和负面性凸显了迫切需要在社交媒体平台上运作的、根据文化量身定制的反信息策略。

Social Media and HPV Vaccine Hesitancy: How Misinformation Affects Latino Parents

Authors: Abraham Aragones, Denise Bruno, Ilona Fridman, Isabella Sarria, Rachel Loia, Claudia Pallasa
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine coverage in the United States remains suboptimal, with Latino communities facing disproportionately lower rates and a higher burden of HPV-associated cancers. Health misinformation and disinformation (M&D) on social media represents a barrier to HPV vaccine uptake, yet little empirical evidence exists on how such content shapes vaccine decision-making. We conducted a cross-sectional study of 98 Latino parents drawn from two randomized, community-based prospective trials conducted across four U.S. Eligible participants confirmed their HPV vaccine-eligible child remained unvaccinated and reported active social media use. A structured survey assessed platform use, access frequency, health information-seeking behaviors, and exposure to HPV vaccine-specific social media content. All 98 parents reported regular social media use. Facebook (68%), Instagram (61%), WhatsApp (61%), and YouTube (48%) were the most used platforms. Three-quarters of parents (77%, n = 75) reported vaccine-related social media exposure in the prior year, with 99% (n = 74) characterizing it as negative. Over half (52%, n = 51) recalled HPV vaccine-specific content, all of whom described it as negative. 41% of all parents (n = 40) reported that perceived negative social media content directly influenced their decision to delay or refuse HPV vaccination; this proportion rose to 78% among those with HPV-specific negative exposure. Social media M&D is a self-reported, substantial driver of HPV vaccine hesitancy among Latino parents. Passive exposure and negativity of vaccine content encountered underscore an urgent need for culturally tailored counter-messaging strategies that operate within social media platforms.

Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine coverage in the United States remains suboptimal, with Latino communities facing disproportionately lower rates and a higher burden of HPV-associated cancers.
Design and population
Over half (52%, n = 51) recalled HPV vaccine-specific content, all of whom described it as negative.
Core methods
We conducted a cross-sectional study of 98 Latino parents drawn from two randomized, community-based prospective trials conducted across four U.S.
Main results
Three-quarters of parents (77%, n = 75) reported vaccine-related social media exposure in the prior year, with 99% (n = 74) characterizing it as negative.
Interpretation and novelty
41% of all parents (n = 40) reported that perceived negative social media content directly influenced their decision to delay or refuse HPV vaccination; this proportion rose to 78% among those with HPV-specific negative exposure.
Scientific and public-health significance
Passive exposure and negativity of vaccine content encountered underscore an urgent need for culturally tailored counter-messaging strategies that operate within social media platforms.
Limitations and evidence strength
Facebook (68%), Instagram (61%), WhatsApp (61%), and YouTube (48%) were the most used platforms.

HPV 阳性角化鼻咽癌与更好的生存相关:NCDB 研究

HPV ‐Positive Keratinizing Nasopharyngeal Carcinoma Is Associated With Better Survival: A NCDB Study
作者： Hannah Chen, Trevor Bowman, David O'Neil Danis, Kenneth Kwan Ho Chui, Basit Jawad, Miriam O'Leary, Jeremiah Tracy, Christian Soneru
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 在鼻咽癌 (NPC) 中的预后相关性。作者试图解决有关 HPV 对鼻咽癌患者生存结果影响的知识差距。
设计与对象
利用国家癌症数据库(NCDB)对 2010 年至 2017 年间鼻咽癌患者和现有 HPV 检测数据进行回顾性分析。该研究纳入了 5081 名患者,其中 3177 名(62.5%)接受了 HPV 检测。
核心方法
使用逻辑回归来预测与 HPV 检测相关的因素,并进行 Kaplan Meier 分析来评估 HPV 阳性和 HPV 阴性 队列研究 之间的总生存期 (OS)。应用 Cox 比例风险模型对影响 OS 的因素进行多变量分析。
主要结果
Kaplan Meier analysis revealed improved overall survival (OS) among HPV-positive patients with keratinizing subtype of NPC (log rank p < 0.001). On multivariate analysis of the keratinizing SCC cohort, HPV positivity was associated with better OS after controlling for various factors (HR 0.81, 95% CI: 0.70-0.94, p = 0.006).
解释与创新
作者解释说,HPV 状态与 NPC 角化亚型患者的 OS 改善独立相关,强调了 HPV 与这一特定组织学亚组中生存结果之间的新关联。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能表明在 NPC 检查中需要进行 HPV 检测,特别是对于角化亚型患者,以指导预后和 治疗 决策。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对现有数据的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

HPV ‐Positive Keratinizing Nasopharyngeal Carcinoma Is Associated With Better Survival: A NCDB Study

Authors: Hannah Chen, Trevor Bowman, David O'Neil Danis, Kenneth Kwan Ho Chui, Basit Jawad, Miriam O'Leary, Jeremiah Tracy, Christian Soneru
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: To investigate the prognostic relevance of human papillomavirus (HPV) in nasopharyngeal carcinoma (NPC). METHODS: The National Cancer Data Base (NCDB) was used to query patients with NPC and available HPV testing data between 2010 and 2017. Logistic regression was performed to predict factors associated with HPV testing and HPV positivity. Overall survival (OS) was evaluated using Kaplan Meier analysis between HPV-positive versus HPV-negative cohorts. Univariate and multivariate analyses using Cox proportional hazards modeling were performed to assess for factors associated with OS. RESULTS: Among our cohort of 5081 patients, 3177 (62.5%) underwent HPV testing. Kaplan Meier analysis revealed improved OS among HPV-positive patients with keratinizing subtype of NPC (log rank p < 0.001). No differences in OS were observed in patients with other histologic subtypes of NPC. On multivariate analysis of the keratinizing SCC cohort, HPV positivity was associated better OS after controlling for sex, age, race, insurance status, facility volume, facility type, zip code level education, Charlson comorbidity score, staging, and treatment (HR 0.81, 95% CI: 0.70-0.94, p = 0.006). CONCLUSION: HPV status was independently associated with improved OS among patients with keratinizing subtype of NPC. Incorporation of HPV testing may be warranted in the workup of NPC.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the prognostic relevance of human papillomavirus (HPV) in nasopharyngeal carcinoma (NPC). The authors sought to address a knowledge gap regarding the impact of HPV on survival outcomes in NPC patients.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis using the National Cancer Data Base (NCDB) was conducted on patients with NPC and available HPV testing data between 2010 and 2017. The study included 5081 patients, of whom 3177 (62.5%) underwent HPV testing.
Core methods
Logistic regression was used to predict factors associated with HPV testing, and Kaplan Meier analysis was performed to evaluate overall survival (OS) between HPV-positive and HPV-negative cohorts. Cox proportional hazards modeling was applied for multivariate analysis of factors influencing OS.
Main results
Kaplan Meier analysis revealed improved overall survival (OS) among HPV-positive patients with keratinizing subtype of NPC (log rank p < 0.001). On multivariate analysis of the keratinizing SCC cohort, HPV positivity was associated with better OS after controlling for various factors (HR 0.81, 95% CI: 0.70-0.94, p = 0.006).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that HPV status was independently associated with improved OS among patients with keratinizing subtype of NPC, highlighting a novel association between HPV and survival outcomes in this specific histologic subgroup.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform the need for HPV testing in the workup of NPC, particularly for patients with keratinizing subtypes, to guide prognosis and treatment decisions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on existing data, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

土耳其一所大学医院医护人员的 HPV 知识、疫苗接种 的吸收和 疫苗接种 的障碍:一项横断面研究

HPV Knowledge, Vaccination Uptake, and Barriers to Vaccination among Healthcare Workers at a University Hospital in Türkiye: A Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Ugur Demirkol, Giray Kolcu, Funda Yıldırım Baş

HPV Knowledge, Vaccination Uptake, and Barriers to Vaccination among Healthcare Workers at a University Hospital in Türkiye: A Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Ugur Demirkol, Giray Kolcu, Funda Yıldırım Baş
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Healthcare workers play a central role in human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination counselling and substantially influence vaccine acceptance. However, adequate theoretical knowledge does not necessarily translate into personal vaccination uptake or effective preventive behaviors. Objective This study aimed to assess HPV-related knowledge, personal HPV vaccination uptake, self-reported barriers to vaccination, and factors independently associated with HPV knowledge among healthcare workers at a university hospital in Türkiye. Methods This descriptive cross-sectional study was conducted at Süleyman Demirel University Research and Application Hospital between 15 December 2025 and 15 February 2026. A total of 288 healthcare workers participated in the study. Data were collected using a sociodemographic questionnaire and a 33-item Turkish HPV Knowledge Scale. Group differences were evaluated using Mann–Whitney U and Kruskal–Wallis tests, followed by Dwass–Steel–Critchlow–Fligner post-hoc comparisons where appropriate. Effect sizes were reported as r and epsilon squared (ε2). Factors independently associated with total HPV knowledge scores were examined using multivariable linear regression with HC3 heteroskedasticity-robust standard errors. Results The mean age of the participants was 36.38 ± 8.62 years, 70.1% were female, and 45.5% were physicians. The mean total HPV Knowledge Scale score was 24.62 ± 6.11 (out of 33). Knowledge gaps were particularly evident regarding vaccine availability and access, age-related indications, and male vaccination. Total knowledge scores differed significantly across the professional groups (H(4) = 123.02, p

阴茎癌的致癌机制:HPV、炎症和其他途径

Carcinogenic mechanisms in penile cancer: HPV, inflammation, and other pathways
作者： Leonardo O. Reis, Luciana S.B. Dal Col, Pablo A.L. Mattos, Heveline R. M. Hoesch, Marina S. Folguieri, Diego M. Capibaribe, Natália D. Avilez, Marcos Tobias-Machado
摘要中文翻译目的:阴茎癌虽然罕见,但会带来深远的临床和社会心理后果。其 诊断 通常发生在晚期,导致残肢手术和生活质量严重受损。大约 95% 的阴茎恶性肿瘤是鳞状细胞癌 (SCC),全球癌症特异性 5 年生存率约为 65%。最近的进展已将注意力从纯粹的肿瘤学结果转向了解其分子异质性和发病机制。越来越多的证据支持涉及病毒和非病毒途径的双重致癌模型,分别由 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染和慢性炎症性皮肤病驱动。该研究的目的是综合有关 HPV 相关和 HPV 独立阴茎 SCC 的分子、免疫学和表观遗传机制的最新证据,强调它们的流行病学相关性、致病相互作用和临床意义。材料和方法:对流行病学、分子和免疫学研究进行严格评估,并根据牛津分类的证据级别和推荐强度对证据进行分级。在 PubMed/MEDLINE、Scopus、Web of Science 和 Literatura Latino-Americana e do Caribe em Ciências da Saúde 中进行了系统检索,涵盖 2000 年 1 月至 2025 年 10 月的出版物。检索词包括(“阴茎癌”或“阴茎癌”或“阴茎鳞状细胞癌”或“阴茎癌”)AND(“HPV”或“人乳头瘤病毒”或“硬化性苔藓”或“龟头炎”或“慢性炎症”或“癌变”或“分子机制”或“肿瘤微环境”或“表观遗传学”或“非编码RNA”或“APOBEC”或“免疫检查点”或“p16”或“TP53”或“E6癌蛋白”或“E7癌蛋白”或“p16INK4a”或“病毒整合”或“HPV DNA”或“E6/E7 mRNA”或“pRb失活”或“p53降解”或“CDKN2A甲基化”或“DNA甲基化”或“组蛋白修饰”或“miRNA”或“lncRNA”或“piRNA”或“癌症相关成纤维细胞”或“肿瘤相关巨噬细胞”或“免疫检查点”或“PD-L1”或“EBV”或“HSV”或“病毒”共同感染”)。根据预先确定的纳入标准(系统评价、临床试验和具有组织学确认的 队列研究 研究)和排除标准(病例报告,结果系列:两种不同但偶尔重叠的致癌机制驱动阴茎癌)选择研究。HPV 相关途径导致 30-50% 的病例,主要与 HPV-16 和 HPV-18 相关。病毒癌蛋白 E6 和 E7 促进 p53 降解和视网膜母细胞瘤 (pRb) 失活,导致增殖失控和 p16^INK4a^ 过度表达。病毒 DNA 整合到 宿主 基因组 中会破坏调节基因并诱导表观遗传改变,包括 CDKN2A 的高甲基化和整体低甲基化。HPV 阳性肿瘤通常表现出较低的突变负担、较高的免疫浸润和改善的预后。尽管如此,HPV 驱动的病变显示出免疫抑制细胞(FoxP3+ 调节性 T 细胞和 CD163+ 巨噬细胞)的富集,以及免疫检查点(程序性细胞死亡 1、淋巴细胞激活基因 3、T 细胞免疫球蛋白和粘蛋白结构域 3)的上调,有利于免疫耗竭和肿瘤持续存在。相反,HPV 独立途径主要源于慢性炎症和皮肤病,例如硬化性苔藓和龟头炎。持续的炎症会产生氧化应激和 DNA 损伤,驱动肿瘤蛋白 53 (TP53) 突变 并激活核因子 kappa B、环氧合酶-2、白细胞介素 6/信号转导器和转录激活剂 3,以及转化生长因子-β 信号级联。这些分子事件建立了一个以血管生成、上皮间质转化和免疫逃避为特征的肿瘤促进微环境。遗传易感性通过 DNA 修复(TP53、乳腺癌基因 2、切除修复交叉互补 4)和免疫调节基因的多态性调节个体风险。此外,非编码RNA(miR-21、miR-223、miR-744-5p)的失调、APOBEC3介导的诱变和病毒联合感染(单纯疱疹病毒、Epstein-Barr病毒)会导致基因组不稳定和疾病进展。结论:阴茎癌发生是一个多因素过程,涉及病毒、炎症和 宿主 基因相互作用,这些相互作用集中在免疫逃避和基因组不稳定上。HPV 驱动的通路和炎症介导的通路可能协同作用,通过共享的炎症和表观遗传回路放大致癌潜力。将 HPV 检测、p16 免疫组织化学和分子分析整合到临床实践中,有助于准确分类、完善预后以及制定精准预防和 治疗 策略。专注于 HPV 疫苗接种、早期包皮环切和慢性炎症管理的公共卫生工作对于减少 发病率 和 死亡率 仍然至关重要,特别是在拉丁美洲和撒哈拉以南非洲等高负担地区。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
HPV 阳性肿瘤通常表现出较低的突变负担、较高的免疫浸润和改善的预后。
设计与对象
研究是根据预先确定的纳入标准(系统评价、临床试验和具有组织学确认的 队列研究 研究)和排除标准(病例报告,结果系列:两种不同但偶尔重叠的致癌机制驱动阴茎癌)进行选择。
核心方法
材料和方法:对流行病学、分子和免疫学研究进行严格评估,并根据牛津分类的证据级别和推荐强度对证据进行分级。
主要结果
HPV 相关途径占 30-50% 的病例,主要与 HPV-16 和 HPV-18 相关。
解释与创新
阴茎癌发生是一个多因素过程,涉及病毒、炎症和 宿主 基因相互作用,这些相互作用集中在免疫逃避和基因组不稳定上。
科研与公卫意义
将 HPV 检测、p16 免疫组织化学和分子分析整合到临床实践中,有助于准确分类、完善预后以及制定精准预防和 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
最近的进展已将注意力从纯粹的肿瘤学结果转向了解其分子异质性和发病机制。

Carcinogenic mechanisms in penile cancer: HPV, inflammation, and other pathways

Authors: Leonardo O. Reis, Luciana S.B. Dal Col, Pablo A.L. Mattos, Heveline R. M. Hoesch, Marina S. Folguieri, Diego M. Capibaribe, Natália D. Avilez, Marcos Tobias-Machado
Original abstract (English)
Objective: Penile cancer, though rare, carries profound clinical and psychosocial consequences. Its diagnosis often occurs at advanced stages, leading to mutilating surgeries and severe impairment in quality of life. Approximately 95% of penile malignancies are squamous cell carcinomas (SCCs), with a global 5-year cancer-specific survival rate of ~65%. Recent advances have shifted attention from purely oncologic outcomes toward understanding its molecular heterogeneity and etiopathogenesis. Growing evidence supports a dual carcinogenic model involving viral and non-viral pathways, driven respectively by human papillomavirus (HPV) infection and chronic inflammatory dermatoses. The objectives of the study are to synthesize current evidence on the molecular, immunologic, and epigenetic mechanisms underlying HPV-related and HPV-independent penile SCC, emphasizing their epidemiologic relevance, pathogenic interplay, and clinical implications. Materials and Methods: A critical appraisal of epidemiological, molecular, and immunological studies was conducted, and evidence was graded according to the Oxford classification for levels of evidence and strength of recommendation. Systematic searches were conducted in PubMed/MEDLINE, Scopus, Web of Science, and Literatura Latino-Americana e do Caribe em Ciências da Saúde, covering publications from January 2000 to October 2025. Search terms included (“penile cancer” OR “penile carcinoma” OR “penile squamous cell carcinoma” OR “cancer of the penis”) AND (“HPV” OR “human papillomavirus” OR “lichen sclerosus” OR “balanitis” OR “chronic inflammation” OR “carcinogenesis” OR “molecular mechanism” OR “tumor microenvironment” OR “epigenetics” OR “non-coding RNA” OR “APOBEC” OR “immune checkpoint” OR “p16” OR “TP53” OR “E6 oncoprotein” OR “E7 oncoprotein” OR “p16INK4a” OR “viral integration” OR “HPV DNA” OR “E6/E7 mRNA” OR “pRb inactivation” OR “p53 degradation” OR “CDKN2A methylation” OR “DNA methylation” OR “histone modification” OR “miRNA” OR “lncRNA” OR “piRNA” OR “cancer-associated fibroblast” OR “tumor-associated macrophage” OR “immune checkpoint” OR “PD-L1” OR “EBV” OR “HSV” OR “viral co-infection”). Studies were selected according to predefined inclusion criteria (systematic reviews, clinical trials, and cohort studies with histological confirmation) and exclusion criteria (case reports, series with Results: Two distinct but occasionally overlapping oncogenic mechanisms drive penile carcinogenesis. The HPV-related pathway, responsible for 30–50% of cases, is predominantly associated with HPV-16 and HPV-18. Viral oncoproteins E6 and E7 promote p53 degradation and retinoblastoma (pRb) inactivation, resulting in uncontrolled proliferation and p16^INK4a^ overexpression. Integration of viral DNA into the host genome disrupts regulatory genes and induces epigenetic alterations, including hypermethylation of CDKN2A and global hypomethylation. HPV-positive tumors typically exhibit lower mutational burden, higher immune infiltration, and improved prognosis. Nonetheless, HPV-driven lesions display enrichment of immunosuppressive cells (FoxP3+ regulatory T cells and CD163+ macrophages), and upregulation of immune checkpoints (programmed cell death 1, lymphocyte activation gene-3, T cell immunoglobulin and mucin-domain containing-3), favoring immune exhaustion and tumor persistence. Conversely, the HPV-independent pathway arises primarily from chronic inflammatory and dermatologic conditions such as lichen sclerosus and balanitis. Persistent inflammation generates oxidative stress and DNA damage, driving tumor protein 53 (TP53) mutations and activating nuclear factor kappa B, cyclooxygenase-2, interleukin 6/signal transducer and activator of transcription 3, and transforming growth factor-beta signaling cascades. These molecular events establish a tumor-promoting microenvironment characterized by angiogenesis, epithelial-mesenchymal transition, and immune evasion. Genetic susceptibility through polymorphisms in DNA repair (TP53, breast cancer gene 2, excision repair cross-complementing 4) and immune-regulatory genes modulates individual risk. In addition, dysregulation of non-coding RNAs (miR-21, miR-223, miR-744-5p), APOBEC3-mediated mutagenesis, and viral co-infections (herpes simplex virus, Epstein-Barr virus) contribute to genomic instability and disease progression. Conclusion: Penile carcinogenesis represents a multifactorial process involving viral, inflammatory, and host-genetic interactions that converge on immune evasion and genomic instability. HPV-driven and inflammation-mediated pathways may act synergistically, amplifying oncogenic potential through shared inflammatory and epigenetic circuits. Integration of HPV testing, p16 immunohistochemistry, and molecular profiling into clinical practice is instrumental for accurate classification, prognostic refinement, and the development of precision-based prevention and treatment strategies. Public health efforts focused on HPV vaccination, early circumcision, and management of chronic inflammatory conditions remain critical to reducing incidence and mortality, particularly in high-burden regions such as Latin America and sub-Saharan Africa.

Interpretation and novelty

在种族限制的观察性 宿主 中,HPV 合并感染负担、阴道微生物群重组和 宿主 背景与宫颈癌前风险相关

HPV co-infection burden, vaginal microbiota restructuring, and host context are associated with Cervical precancer risk in a racially restricted observational cohort
作者： Katherine Y. Tossas, Bin Zhu, Katarzyna M. Tyc, Ciara Rhodes, Jerome F. Strauss III, Myrna G. Serrano, Gregory A. Buck, III JFS, Jerome F. Strauss
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是宫颈癌发生所必需的,但单独检测 HPV 并不能区分短暂性感染和进展风险最大的病变。
设计与对象
我们评估了阴道微生物组健康项目 (VaMHP) 的黑人/非裔美国人和白人分析 队列研究 中 HPV 负荷、阴道微生物群结构和 宿主 上下文变量是否共同表征了 3 级宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN3),整合了基于 L1 的 HPV 分型、16S rRNA 阴道微生物群分析和相关临床元数据。
核心方法
Among 1181 participants, 75 had CIN3. CIN3 was associated with HPV positivity (55/75, 73.3% vs 431/1106, 39.0%; odds ratio [OR] 4.31, 95% CI 2.55–7.29; Fisher exact p = 7.9 x 10^-9) and with multiple HPV infection among HPV-positive participants (35/55, 63.6% vs 176/431, 40.8%; OR 2.54, 95% CI 1.42–4.54; p = 0.0022).
主要结果
CIN3 阳性样本中的 HPV 群落表现出更高的香农多样性、更大的观察到的菌株丰富度、更高的均匀度和显着的 β 多样性分离。
解释与创新
在阴道微生物群分析中,α多样性并不因 CIN3 状态而异,但群落组成却不同,并且卷曲乳杆菌是经过多重测试校正后 CIN3 中唯一被耗尽的分类群。
科研与公卫意义
种族、年龄和甲硝唑暴露是 宿主 因子网络的中心节点。在预测建模中,结合元数据、HPV 和阴道微生物群特征的完整集成模型 (auROC = 0.745) 优于 HPV + 阴道微生物群 (auROC = 0.670) 和仅 HPV (auROC = 0.440) 模型。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告研究设计的具体局限性。

HPV co-infection burden, vaginal microbiota restructuring, and host context are associated with Cervical precancer risk in a racially restricted observational cohort

Authors: Katherine Y. Tossas, Bin Zhu, Katarzyna M. Tyc, Ciara Rhodes, Jerome F. Strauss III, Myrna G. Serrano, Gregory A. Buck, III JFS, Jerome F. Strauss
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background High-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is necessary for cervical carcinogenesis, but HPV detection alone does not distinguish transient infection from lesions at greatest risk of progression. We evaluated whether HPV burden, vaginal microbiota structure, and host-context variables jointly characterize cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 3 (CIN3) in a Black/African American and White analytic cohort from the Vaginal Microbiome Health Project (VaMHP), integrating L1-based HPV typing, 16S rRNA vaginal microbiota profiling, and linked clinical metadata. Results Among 1181 participants, 75 had CIN3. CIN3 was associated with HPV positivity (55/75, 73.3% vs 431/1106, 39.0%; odds ratio [OR] 4.31, 95% CI 2.55–7.29; Fisher exact p = 7.9 x 10^-9) and with multiple HPV infection among HPV-positive participants (35/55, 63.6% vs 176/431, 40.8%; OR 2.54, 95% CI 1.42–4.54; p = 0.0022). HPV communities in CIN3-positive samples showed higher Shannon diversity, greater observed strain richness, higher evenness, and significant beta-diversity separation. In vaginal microbiota analyses, alpha diversity did not differ by CIN3 status, but community composition did, and Lactobacillus crispatus was the only taxon depleted in CIN3 after multiple-testing correction. Race, age, and metronidazole exposure were central nodes in the host-factor network. In predictive modeling, a full integrated model combining metadata, HPV, and vaginal microbiota features (auROC = 0.745) outperformed both HPV + vaginal microbiota (auROC = 0.670) and HPV-only (auROC = 0.440) models. Conclusions CIN3 in this cohort was associated with coordinated shifts in virologic burden, vaginal community structure, and host social-clinical context. The results support a structure-function interpretation in which loss of Lactobacillus crispatus-dominant states and enrichment of dysbiosis-associated communities define a host-microbiome context that is more permissive to HPV persistence and precancer. These findings move beyond descriptive omics by showing that microbiome and host-context features add nonredundant discriminatory signal beyond HPV-only models.

Question and background
High-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is necessary for cervical carcinogenesis, but HPV detection alone does not distinguish transient infection from lesions at greatest risk of progression.
Design and population

Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design.

中国乐山接受宫颈筛查的女性的高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 基因型:实时和巢式多重 PCR

High-risk Human Papillomavirus Genotypes in Women Undergoing Cervical Screening in Leshan, China: Real-time and Nested Multiplex PCR
作者： Li-Hua Xia, Ye Ye
摘要中文翻译高危人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)感染是宫颈癌前病变和宫颈癌的主要病因。HR-HPV 监测 的区域 监测 和基因型分布很重要,因为循环基因型可能根据地理位置、年龄结构、筛查途径和 疫苗接种 覆盖范围而变化。这项回顾性横断面研究评估了 2022 年 1 月至 2024 年 12 月期间在中国四川省乐山市妇幼保健院接受常规 宫颈癌 筛查的女性的 HR-HPV 感染情况。总共纳入了 5,000 名年龄在 25-60 岁之间的合格女性。使用无菌细胞刷从转化区收集宫颈标本并保存在液基细胞学培养基中。主要 HR-HPV 检测使用 Roche Cobas 4800 HPV 检测进行,该检测可单独检测 HPV16 和 HPV18,并检测 12 种其他高危基因型作为汇总结果。通过巢式多重 PCR 进一步分析合并 HR-HPV 阳性的样本,以确定其他基因型。对细胞学和组织病理学结果(如有)进行了审查,并将其与 HPV 检测结果相关联。在 5,000 名接受筛查的女性中,612 名 HR-HPV 呈阳性,相当于 患病率 的总体比例为 12.2%。HPV阳性病例中,单基因型感染占65.7%,多基因型感染占34.3%。HPV16 是最常见的基因型,其次是 HPV31、HPV56、HPV66 和 HPV51。HR-HPV 患病率 在 25-30 岁女性中最高,并随着年龄的增长而下降。多变量分析显示,年龄低于 35 岁、多个性伴侣、吸烟和缺乏事先筛查与 HR-HPV 阳性的几率较高相关。这些发现描述了乐山宫颈筛查 队列研究 中 HR-HPV 感染的区域 流行病学,并支持持续的基因型 监测,而由于 疫苗接种 历史数据不完整,有关基因型替换和 疫苗 影响的结论应谨慎解释。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
高危人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)感染是宫颈癌前病变和宫颈癌的主要病因。本研究的重点是在中国乐山接受宫颈筛查的女性中 HR-HPV 类型及其与 宫颈癌 的关联。
证据基础与方法
这项回顾性横断面研究评估了 2022 年 1 月至 2024 年 12 月期间在中国四川省乐山市妇幼保健院接受常规 宫颈癌 筛查的女性的 HR-HPV 感染情况。总共纳入了 5,000 名年龄在 25-60 岁之间的合格女性。使用无菌细胞刷从转化区收集宫颈标本并保存在液基细胞学培养基中。主要 HR-HPV 检测使用 Roche Cobas 4800 HPV 检测进行,该检测可单独检测 HPV16 和 HPV18,并检测 12 种其他高危基因型作为汇总结果。通过巢式多重 PCR 进一步分析合并 HR-HPV 阳性的样本,以确定其他基因型。
共识与结论
在 5,000 名接受筛查的女性中,612 名 HR-HPV 呈阳性,相当于 患病率 的总体比例为 12.2%。HPV阳性病例中,单基因型感染占65.7%,多基因型感染占34.3%。HPV16 是最常见的基因型,其次是 HPV31、HPV56、HPV66 和 HPV51。HR-HPV 患病率 在 25-30 岁女性中最高,并随着年龄的增长而下降。多变量分析显示,年龄低于 35 岁、多个性伴侣、吸烟和缺乏事先筛查与 HR-HPV 阳性的几率较高相关。
争议与缺口
这些发现描述了乐山宫颈筛查 队列研究 中 HR-HPV 感染的区域 流行病学,并支持持续的基因型 监测,而由于 疫苗接种 历史数据不完整,有关基因型替换和 疫苗 影响的结论应谨慎解释。
科研与实践启示
HR-HPV 监测 的区域 监测 和基因型分布很重要,因为循环基因型可能根据地理位置、年龄结构、筛查途径和 疫苗接种 覆盖范围而变化。

High-risk Human Papillomavirus Genotypes in Women Undergoing Cervical Screening in Leshan, China: Real-time and Nested Multiplex PCR

Authors: Li-Hua Xia, Ye Ye
Original abstract (English)
High-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection is the principal etiological factor in cervical precancer and cervical cancer. Regional surveillance of HR-HPV prevalence and genotype distribution is important because circulating genotypes may vary according to geography, age structure, screening access, and vaccination coverage. This retrospective cross-sectional study evaluated HR-HPV infection among women undergoing routine cervical cancer screening at Leshan Maternal and Child Health Hospital, Leshan, Sichuan, China, between January 2022 and December 2024. A total of 5,000 eligible women aged 25-60 years were included. Cervical specimens were collected from the transformation zone using a sterile cytobrush and preserved in liquid-based cytology medium. Primary HR-HPV detection was performed using the Roche Cobas 4800 HPV assay, which detects HPV16 and HPV18 individually and detects 12 additional high-risk genotypes as a pooled result. Samples positive for pooled HR-HPV were further analyzed by nested multiplex PCR to identify additional genotypes. Cytology and histopathology results, where available, were reviewed and correlated with HPV detection results. Among 5,000 screened women, 612 were HR-HPV positive, corresponding to an overall prevalence of 12.2%. Single-genotype infections accounted for 65.7% of HPV-positive cases, while multiple-genotype infections accounted for 34.3%. HPV16 was the most frequent genotype, followed by HPV31, HPV56, HPV66, and HPV51. HR-HPV prevalence was highest in women aged 25-30 years and declined with increasing age. Multivariate analysis showed that age below 35 years, multiple sexual partners, smoking, and lack of prior screening were associated with higher odds of HR-HPV positivity. These findings describe the regional epidemiology of HR-HPV infection in a cervical screening cohort from Leshan and support continued genotype surveillance, while conclusions regarding genotype replacement and vaccine impact should be interpreted with caution due to incomplete vaccination history data.

中国东部宁波市鄞州中学生在人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种决策中的角色演变:一项以教育行业为影响力参与者的定性研究

Evolving Roles in Decision-Making About Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Among Middle School Girls in Yinzhou of Ningbo, Eastern China: A Qualitative Study With a Focus on the Educational Sector as an Influential Player
作者： Zilian Yu, Xiaoya Fu, Bei Zhou, Shengbo Zhang, Linfei Zhu, Weiyu Zhou, Yihan Lu
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 得到世界卫生组织认可用于 宫颈癌 预防,但不属于中国国家免疫规划的一部分。本研究探讨了不同关键利益相关者在 HPV 疫苗接种 决策中的作用,重点关注中国东部宁波市鄞州的一个针对初中一年级女生的当地试点项目。数据收集于 2023 年 10 月 18 日至 11 月 14 日,通过文献和政策回顾以及与当地主要利益相关者的深入访谈和焦点小组讨论。使用社会生态模型 (SEM) 进行主题分析,以确定影响 HPV 疫苗接种 五个级别决策的决定因素。共有 55 名参与者接受了采访。结果表明,教育部门在该计划的设计和实施过程中与卫生当局密切合作。尽管直接效益有限,但中学和教师在影响家长和学生方面发挥了重要作用。在人际层面,教育工作者传播疫苗相关信息,并对家长和学生进行引导。在组织层面,教育和卫生部门之间以及教师和医疗保健专业人员之间的合作对决策产生了强烈影响。在社区层面,向教师提供的健康教育延伸至家长和学生,提高了认识和支持。研究结果强调了教育部门在促进 HPV 疫苗接种 方面的关键作用,并强调了学校积极支持以及与其他主要利益相关者建立有效伙伴关系的重要性。这些见解可能适用于考虑类似 HPV 疫苗接种 举措的中国其他社会经济发达地区。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 得到世界卫生组织认可用于 宫颈癌 预防,但不属于中国国家免疫规划的一部分。本研究探讨了不同关键利益相关者在 HPV 疫苗接种 决策中的作用,重点关注中国东部宁波市鄞州的一个针对初中一年级女生的当地试点项目。
证据基础与方法
数据收集于 2023 年 10 月 18 日至 11 月 14 日,通过文献和政策回顾以及与当地主要利益相关者的深入访谈和焦点小组讨论。使用社会生态模型 (SEM) 进行主题分析,以确定影响 HPV 疫苗接种 五个级别决策的决定因素。
共识与结论
结果表明,教育部门在该计划的设计和实施过程中与卫生当局密切合作。尽管直接效益有限,但中学和教师在影响家长和学生方面发挥了重要作用。在人际层面,教育工作者传播疫苗相关信息,并对家长和学生进行引导。在组织层面,教育和卫生部门之间以及教师和医疗保健专业人员之间的合作对决策产生了强烈影响。在社区层面,向教师提供的健康教育延伸至家长和学生,提高了认识和支持。
争议与缺口
研究结果强调了教育部门在促进 HPV 疫苗接种 方面的关键作用,并强调了学校积极支持以及与其他主要利益相关者建立有效伙伴关系的重要性。这些见解可能适用于考虑类似 HPV 疫苗接种 举措的中国其他社会经济发达地区。
科研与实践启示
研究表明,教育部门在 HPV 疫苗接种 计划中发挥着重要作用。未来的研究应侧重于了解教育部门为增强家长和学生对 疫苗 的吸收和支持而采用的具体机制和策略。实际影响包括教育和卫生部门之间需要继续合作,以确保 HPV 疫苗接种 计划的有效实施。

Evolving Roles in Decision-Making About Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Among Middle School Girls in Yinzhou of Ningbo, Eastern China: A Qualitative Study With a Focus on the Educational Sector as an Influential Player

Authors: Zilian Yu, Xiaoya Fu, Bei Zhou, Shengbo Zhang, Linfei Zhu, Weiyu Zhou, Yihan Lu
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination is endorsed by the World Health Organization for cervical cancer prevention, but it is not part of China’s national immunization program. This study explores the roles of various key interest holders in HPV vaccination decisions, focusing on a local pilot program in Yinzhou, Ningbo, eastern China, targeting first-year middle school girls. Data were collected from October 18 to November 14, 2023, using literature and policy reviews and in-depth interviews and focus group discussions with local key interest holders. Thematic analysis was performed using a Social Ecological Model (SEM) to identify determinants influencing HPV vaccination decisions across five levels. A total of 55 participants were interviewed. The results showed that the educational sector worked closely with health authorities in the program’s design and implementation. Middle schools and teachers played a significant role in influencing both parents and students, despite limited direct benefits. At the interpersonal level, educators disseminated vaccine-related information and guided parents and students. At the organizational level, the collaboration between the educational and health sectors, as well as between teachers and health care professionals, strongly influenced decision-making. At the community level, health education provided to teachers extended to parents and students, enhancing awareness and support. The findings highlight the crucial role of educational sectors in promoting HPV vaccination and emphasize the importance of positive support from schools and effective partnerships with other key stakeholders. These insights may be applicable to other socioeconomically advanced regions in China considering similar HPV vaccination initiatives.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination is endorsed by the World Health Organization for cervical cancer prevention, but it is not part of China’s national immunization program. This study explores the roles of various key interest holders in HPV vaccination decisions, focusing on a local pilot program in Yinzhou, Ningbo, eastern China, targeting first-year middle school girls.
Evidence base and method
Data were collected from October 18 to November 14, 2023, using literature and policy reviews and in-depth interviews and focus group discussions with local key interest holders. Thematic analysis was performed using a Social Ecological Model (SEM) to identify determinants influencing HPV vaccination decisions across five levels.
Consensus and conclusions
The results showed that the educational sector worked closely with health authorities in the program’s design and implementation. Middle schools and teachers played a significant role in influencing both parents and students, despite limited direct benefits. At the interpersonal level, educators disseminated vaccine-related information and guided parents and students. At the organizational level, the collaboration between the educational and health sectors, as well as between teachers and health care professionals, strongly influenced decision-making. At the community level, health education provided to teachers extended to parents and students, enhancing awareness and support.
Controversies and gaps
The findings highlight the crucial role of educational sectors in promoting HPV vaccination and emphasize the importance of positive support from schools and effective partnerships with other key stakeholders. These insights may be applicable to other socioeconomically advanced regions in China considering similar HPV vaccination initiatives.
Research and practice implications
The study suggests that educational sectors play a significant role in HPV vaccination programs. Future research should focus on understanding the specific mechanisms and strategies employed by educational sectors to enhance vaccine uptake and support from parents and students. Practical implications include the need for continued collaboration between educational and health sectors to ensure effective implementation of HPV vaccination programs.

银屑病的生物疗法和粘膜 人乳头瘤病毒 感染的结果:系统评价和叙述综合

Biologic therapies for psoriasis and outcomes on mucosal human papillomavirus infections: a systematic review and narrative synthesis
作者： Jonathan Krygier, Bertrand Richert, Charlotte Martin
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染很常见,可能受到用于治疗银屑病的免疫调节生物制剂的影响。肿瘤坏死因子 (TNF)-α、白细胞介素 (IL)-12/23、IL-17 和 IL-23 抑制对粘膜 HPV 结果的影响仍不确定。目的 系统评价接受批准用于治疗银屑病的生物制剂治疗的患者中粘膜 HPV 感染的发生、消退和复发的临床证据,并提供皮肤或间接 HPV 结果的补充性叙述综合。方法 对 PubMed、Embase 和 Scopus 进行系统检索(从成立到 2025 年 10 月),确定了报告接受批准用于治疗银屑病的生物制剂的患者 HPV 结果的研究,无论 治疗 适应症如何。数据提取和质量评估遵循标准化程序。结果 41 项研究符合纳入标准;18 篇讨论了粘膜 HPV 结果,23 篇对叙述性综合做出了贡献。总体方法学质量为低至中等。尽管有零星尖锐湿疣病例报道,但抗 TNF 治疗并未发现人群中肛门生殖器 HPV 风险持续增加。IL-17 抑制剂产生了异质性和环境依赖性的结果,包括口腔 HPV 检测可能增加,但生殖器 HPV 信号没有一致。乌司奴单抗的结果好坏参半,有恶化的情况,也有记录在案的消退的情况。根据非常有限的数据,选择性 IL-23 抑制剂与粘膜 HPV 风险明显增加无关;有孤立回归的报道。生物制剂下的皮肤 HPV 表现并不常见,而且通常是良性的。结论 目前的证据并未表明服用批准用于治疗银屑病的生物制剂的人粘膜 HPV 感染的风险明显增加,尽管可能会出现孤立的恶化。需要包括 CONDYPSO 项目在内的前瞻性研究来阐明因果关系并指导 HPV 疫苗接种 和粘膜 监测 等预防策略。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
接受生物疗法治疗的银屑病患者的 人乳头瘤病毒 感染;该综述旨在确定抑制 TNF-α、IL-12/23、IL-17 和 IL-23 如何影响粘膜 HPV 的发生、消退和复发。
证据基础与方法
从成立到 2025 年 10 月,对 PubMed、Embase 和 Scopus 的系统检索发现了 41 项研究(18 项涉及粘膜 HPV 结果,23 项有助于叙述综合)。数据提取和质量评估遵循标准化程序;总体方法学质量为低至中等。
共识与结论
尽管有散发性尖锐湿疣病例报道,但抗 TNF 治疗并未发现人群中肛门生殖器 HPV 风险持续增加。IL-17 抑制剂产生了异质性、环境依赖性的结果,包括口腔 HPV 检测可能增加,但生殖器信号没有一致。乌司奴单抗显示出不同的结果,既有恶化,也有记录的回归。根据非常有限的数据,选择性 IL-23 抑制剂与粘膜 HPV 风险明显增加无关;有孤立回归的报道。生物制剂下的皮肤 HPV 表现并不常见,而且通常是良性的。
争议与缺口
证据质量为低至中等,有关 IL-23 抑制剂的数据非常有限。IL-17 研究结果的异质性和缺乏明确的因果关系凸显了尚未解决的机制。需要进行前瞻性研究来澄清关联并指导预防策略。
科研与实践启示
需要进行前瞻性研究,例如 CONDYPSO 项目,以澄清因果关系并指导预防策略,包括 HPV 疫苗接种 和粘膜 监测。

Biologic therapies for psoriasis and outcomes on mucosal human papillomavirus infections: a systematic review and narrative synthesis

Authors: Jonathan Krygier, Bertrand Richert, Charlotte Martin
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) infections are common and may be influenced by immunomodulatory biologics used for psoriasis. The influence of tumour necrosis factor (TNF)-α, interleukin (IL)-12/23, IL-17 and IL-23 inhibition on mucosal HPV outcomes remains uncertain. Objectives To systematically review the clinical evidence on the occurrence, regression and recurrence of mucosal HPV infections in patients treated with biologics approved to treat psoriasis, and to provide a complementary narrative synthesis of cutaneous or indirect HPV findings. Methods A systematic search of PubMed, Embase and Scopus (from inception to October 2025) identified studies reporting HPV outcomes in patients receiving biologics approved to treat psoriasis, irrespective of the treatment indication. Data extraction and quality assessment followed standardized procedures. Results Forty-one studies met the inclusion criteria; 18 addressed mucosal HPV outcomes and 23 contributed to the narrative synthesis. The overall methodological quality was low to moderate. No consistent population-level increase in anogenital HPV risk was found with anti-TNF therapy, although sporadic cases of condyloma were reported. IL-17 inhibitors yielded heterogeneous and context-dependent findings, including a possible increase in oral HPV detection, without a consistent genital HPV signal. Ustekinumab showed mixed outcomes, with exacerbations and documented regressions. Selective IL-23 inhibitors were not associated with a clear increased risk of mucosal HPV, based on very limited data; isolated regression has been reported. Cutaneous HPV manifestations under biologics were uncommon and generally benign. Conclusions The current evidence does not indicate a clear increased risk of mucosal HPV infection in people taking biologics approved to treat psoriasis, although isolated exacerbations may occur. Prospective studies, including the CONDYPSO project, are needed to clarify causal links and to guide preventive strategies such as HPV vaccination and mucosal surveillance.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Human papillomavirus infections in patients with psoriasis treated with biologic therapies; the review seeks to determine how inhibition of TNF‐α, IL‐12/23, IL‐17, and IL‐23 influences mucosal HPV occurrence, regression, and recurrence.
Evidence base and method
A systematic search of PubMed, Embase and Scopus from inception to October 2025 identified 41 studies (18 addressing mucosal HPV outcomes and 23 contributing to a narrative synthesis). Data extraction and quality assessment followed standardized procedures; overall methodological quality was low to moderate.
Consensus and conclusions
No consistent population‐level increase in anogenital HPV risk was found with anti‐TNF therapy, although sporadic condyloma cases were reported. IL‐17 inhibitors yielded heterogeneous, context‐dependent findings, including a possible increase in oral HPV detection but no consistent genital signal. Ustekinumab showed mixed outcomes, with both exacerbations and documented regressions. Selective IL‐23 inhibitors were not associated with a clear increased risk of mucosal HPV, based on very limited data; isolated regression has been reported. Cutaneous HPV manifestations under biologics were uncommon and generally benign.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence quality was low to moderate, and data on IL‐23 inhibitors were very limited. Heterogeneity in IL‐17 findings and the absence of clear causal links highlight unresolved mechanisms. Prospective studies are needed to clarify associations and guide preventive strategies.
Research and practice implications
Prospective studies, such as the CONDYPSO project, are required to clarify causal links and to guide preventive strategies, including HPV vaccination and mucosal surveillance.

探索成人幽门螺杆菌与 人乳头瘤病毒 之间的潜在关联:范围界定综述

Exploring the potential association between Helicobacter pylori and Human Papillomavirus in adults: a scoping review
作者： Nicola Serra, Michela Buttà, Virginia Talarico, Alessia Varvarà, Arianna Sucato, Elettra Unti, Paola Di Carlo, Anna Giammanco, Teresa Fasciana, Giuseppina Capra
摘要中文翻译这篇范围界定综述综合了关于成年人群中幽门螺杆菌和 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 之间的潜在关联、解剖分布和生物相互作用的现有文献。根据 PRISMA-ScR(系统评价的首选报告项目和范围评价的荟萃分析扩展)指南,从数据库建立到 2026 年 7 月至 8 月,在 PubMed/MEDLINE、Scopus 和 Web of Science 中进行了系统文献检索。从确定的 58 项初始记录中,有 9 项研究在全面资格筛选后符合纳入标准。分析表明研究设计、组织取样和检测方法存在显着的异质性。尽管在胃和上呼吸消化道组织中报告了幽门螺杆菌和 HPV 的共同检测,但 患病率 的比率在 Z​​XQTERMABQXZ 的研究中差异很大。在胃癌组织中,仅以低频率检测到 HPV,并且没有显示出活跃的致癌驱动活性的功能证据。相反,它表现出更高的 患病率 以及与头颈鳞状细胞癌 (HNSCC) 临床肿瘤特征更强的相关性。幽门螺杆菌在胃部区域的致癌作用已得到证实,但与 HNSCC 的关联性较弱且不太一致。总体而言,目前的证据表明,幽门螺杆菌和 HPV 的共存可能是偶然的,而不是协同作用的。HPV 的临床相关性似乎严格依赖于部位,表明与头颈恶性肿瘤有很大关系,但在胃癌和食道癌中意义有限。需要进一步精心设计的纵向研究结合综合分子和功能分析来明确阐明潜在的病原体串扰。

Exploring the potential association between Helicobacter pylori and Human Papillomavirus in adults: a scoping review

Authors: Nicola Serra, Michela Buttà, Virginia Talarico, Alessia Varvarà, Arianna Sucato, Elettra Unti, Paola Di Carlo, Anna Giammanco, Teresa Fasciana, Giuseppina Capra
Original abstract (English)
This scoping review synthesises existing literature on the potential association, anatomical distribution, and biological interactions between Helicobacter pylori and Human papillomavirus (HPV) in adult populations. Conducted according to PRISMA-ScR (Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses extension for Scoping Reviews) guidelines, a systematic literature search was carried out across PubMed/MEDLINE, Scopus, and Web of Science from database inception throughout July and August 2026. From 58 initial records identified, 9 studies met the inclusion criteria following full eligibility screening. The analysis demonstrates substantial heterogeneity in study design, tissue sampling, and detection methodologies. Although co-detection of H. pylori and HPV was reported across gastric and upper aerodigestive tract tissues, prevalence rates varied widely across study cohorts. In gastric cancer tissues, HPV was detected only at low frequencies and showed no functional evidence of active oncogenic driver activity. Conversely, it exhibited higher prevalence and stronger associations with clinical tumour characteristics in head and neck squamous cell carcinomas (HNSCC). pylori's well-established oncogenic role in the gastric region was accompanied by a weaker and less consistent association with HNSCC. Overall, the current body of evidence indicates that the coexistence of H. pylori and HPV is likely incidental rather than synergistic. The clinical relevance of HPV appears strictly site-dependent, demonstrating substantial involvement in head and neck malignancies but limited significance in gastric and oesophageal cancers. Further well-designed longitudinal studies incorporating integrated molecular and functional analyses are required to clarify potential pathogen crosstalk definitively.

Scope and question
This scoping review synthesises existing literature on the potential association, anatomical distribution, and biological interactions between Helicobacter pylori and Human papillomavirus (HPV) in adult populations.
Evidence base and method
From 58 initial records identified, 9 studies met the inclusion criteria following full eligibility screening.
Consensus and conclusions
Overall, the current body of evidence indicates that the coexistence of H.
Controversies and gaps
The analysis demonstrates substantial heterogeneity in study design, tissue sampling, and detection methodologies.
Research and practice implications
The clinical relevance of HPV appears strictly site-dependent, demonstrating substantial involvement in head and neck malignancies but limited significance in gastric and oesophageal cancers.

致癌病毒的神经并发症:机制、临床表现和新见解。

Neurological Complications of Oncogenic Viruses: Mechanisms, Clinical Manifestations, and Emerging Insights.
作者： Lei Liu
摘要中文翻译致癌病毒因其在恶性转化中的关键作用而被广泛认可。然而,它们引起神经系统疾病的能力相对较少受到关注。越来越多的证据表明,多种致癌病毒,包括人类 T 细胞白血病病毒 1 型 (HTLV-1)、人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV)、乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV)、丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 和卡波西肉瘤相关疱疹病毒 (KSHV),与涉及中枢和周围神经系统的广泛神经系统并发症有关。这些表现是通过多种机制产生的,包括直接病毒神经侵袭、持续感染、免疫介导的组织损伤、慢性神经炎症和神经血管完整性破坏。此外,病毒诱导的分子改变,如氧化应激、线粒体功能障碍、表观遗传重塑和异常的细胞因子信号传导,会导致神经元功能障碍和疾病进展。在这篇综述中,我们通过整合分子、实验和临床研究的证据,批判性地研究了目前对与主要致癌病毒相关的神经并发症的理解。我们首先讨论病毒感染引起神经损伤的一般机制,强调嗜神经病毒和非嗜神经病毒之间共享的途径。然后,我们总结了与每种致癌病毒相关的神经学表现和病毒特异性致病机制,强调神经发病机制中常见和独特的分子途径。最后,我们解决当前的诊断挑战、新兴生物标志物和治疗策略,同时确定限制因果解释和临床转化的关键知识差距。全面了解致癌病毒、免疫系统和神经系统之间复杂的相互作用将有助于早期 诊断,改善患者管理,并支持开发针对病毒相关神经系统疾病的靶向治疗方法。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注与 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关的神经系统并发症,特别研究这些病毒引起神经损伤的机制、其疾病特异性表现以及对 诊断 和 治疗 的影响。
证据基础与方法
该综述整合了分子、实验和临床研究的证据,以严格检查 HPV 的神经系统并发症。它讨论了病毒引起的神经损伤的一般机制,然后总结了病毒特异性的致病机制,强调了常见和独特的分子途径。
共识与结论
该综述的结论是,HPV 可通过多种机制诱发神经系统并发症,包括直接神经侵袭、持续感染、免疫介导的组织损伤、慢性神经炎症和神经血管完整性破坏。这些机制与其他嗜神经病毒和非嗜神经病毒共有。
争议与缺口
该综述确定了限制因果解释和临床转化的关键知识差距,特别是在理解精确的分子途径和特定 HPV 类型在神经系统疾病进展中的作用方面。
科研与实践启示
该综述强调需要进一步研究 HPV、免疫系统和神经系统之间复杂的相互作用,以改进诊断方法、开发有针对性的治疗方法并加强患者管理策略。

Neurological Complications of Oncogenic Viruses: Mechanisms, Clinical Manifestations, and Emerging Insights.

Authors: Lei Liu
Original abstract (English)
Oncogenic viruses are widely recognized for their pivotal role in malignant transformation; however, their capacity to induce neurological disorders has received comparatively less attention. Increasing evidence indicates that several oncogenic viruses, including human T-cell leukemia virus type 1 (HTLV-1), human papillomavirus (HPV), hepatitis B virus (HBV), hepatitis C virus (HCV), and Kaposi's sarcoma-associated herpesvirus (KSHV), are associated with a broad spectrum of neurological complications involving both the central and peripheral nervous systems. These manifestations arise through diverse mechanisms, including direct viral neuroinvasion, persistent infection, immune-mediated tissue injury, chronic neuroinflammation, and disruption of neurovascular integrity. Moreover, virus-induced molecular alterations, such as oxidative stress, mitochondrial dysfunction, epigenetic remodelling, and aberrant cytokine signalling, contribute t neuronal dysfunction and disease progression. In this review, we critically examine the current understanding of the neurological complications associated with major oncogenic viruses by integrating evidence from molecular, experimental, and clinical studies. We first discuss the general mechanisms by which viral infections induce neurological injury, highlighting pathways shared among neurotropic and non-neurotropic viruses. We then summarise the neurological manifestations and virus-specific pathogenic mechanisms associated with each oncogenic virus, emphasising both common and distinct molecular pathways underlying neuropathogenesis. Finally, we address current diagnostic challenges, emerging biomarkers, and therapeutic strategies while identifying critical knowledge gaps that limit causal interpretation and clinical translation. A comprehensive understanding of the complex interactions between oncogenic viruses, the immune system, and the nervous system will facilitate earlier diagnosis, improve patient management, and support the development of targeted therapeutic approaches for virus-associated neurological disorders.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on the neurological complications associated with human papillomaviruses (HPV), specifically examining the mechanisms by which these viruses induce neurological injury, their disease-specific manifestations, and the implications for diagnosis and treatment.
Evidence base and method
The review integrates evidence from molecular, experimental, and clinical studies to critically examine the neurological complications of HPV. It discusses general mechanisms of viral-induced neurological injury and then summarizes virus-specific pathogenic mechanisms, emphasizing both common and distinct molecular pathways.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that HPV can induce neurological complications through various mechanisms, including direct neuroinvasion, persistent infection, immune-mediated tissue injury, chronic neuroinflammation, and disruption of neurovascular integrity. These mechanisms are shared with other neurotropic and non-neurotropic viruses.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies critical knowledge gaps that limit causal interpretation and clinical translation, particularly in understanding the precise molecular pathways and the role of specific HPV types in neurological disease progression.
Research and practice implications
The review highlights the need for further research into the complex interactions between HPV, the immune system, and the nervous system to improve diagnostic methods, develop targeted therapeutic approaches, and enhance patient management strategies.

HPV 疫苗接种 的覆盖范围和残疾人士面临的障碍:范围界定审查

HPV vaccination coverage and barriers for people with disability: A scoping review
作者： Sarah Walmsley, Claire M. Vajdic, Glenda M. Bishop, Robert Long, Zoe Aitken
摘要中文翻译本次范围界定审查探讨了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 的覆盖范围和残疾人士面临的障碍。我们的全球检索确定了 2006 年至 2025 年间发表的 33 项研究,全部来自高收入国家。我们发现了不同国家、不同性别、不同环境和不同残疾群体的残疾人 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率不平等的证据。常见的 疫苗接种 障碍包括对性活动的误解、在主要基于医疗机构的 HPV 疫苗接种 计划设置中缺乏医疗保健专业人员的 疫苗接种 建议以及缺乏员工培训/资源。针对所有残疾人的全人口研究、低收入和中等收入环境的研究以及包括聋哑人或盲人社区或有身体或心理残疾的人的研究都存在明显差距。包容残疾人的研究方法有限,应优先考虑以确保未来的研究满足社区需求。如果要实现 HPV 疫苗接种 目标,必须支持所有人(包括残疾人)公平获得疫苗。

范围与问题
常见的 疫苗接种 障碍包括对性活动的误解、在主要基于医疗机构的 HPV 疫苗接种 计划设置中缺乏医疗保健专业人员的 疫苗接种 建议以及缺乏员工培训/资源。
证据基础与方法
我们的全球检索发现了 33 项研究,全部来自高收入国家,发表时间为 2006 年至 2025 年。
共识与结论
我们发现有证据表明,不同国家、不同性别、不同环境和不同残疾群体的残疾人的 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率存在不平等。
争议与缺口
包容残疾人的研究方法有限,应优先考虑以确保未来的研究满足社区需求。
科研与实践启示
如果要实现 HPV 疫苗接种 目标,必须支持所有人(包括残疾人)公平获得疫苗。

HPV vaccination coverage and barriers for people with disability: A scoping review

Authors: Sarah Walmsley, Claire M. Vajdic, Glenda M. Bishop, Robert Long, Zoe Aitken
Original abstract (English)
This scoping review explored human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination coverage and barriers for people with disability. Our global search identified 33 studies, all from high-income countries, published between 2006 and 2025. We found evidence of inequalities in HPV vaccination coverage for people with disability across a range of countries, sexes/genders, settings, and disability groups. Common vaccination barriers included misassumptions around sexual activity, lack of vaccination recommendations from healthcare professionals in primarily healthcare-facility-based HPV vaccination programme settings, and lack of staff training/resources. There were marked gaps in population-wide research for all people with disability, research from low- and middle-income settings, and research including deaf or blind communities, or people with physical or psychosocial disability. Disability-inclusive research methodologies were limited and should be prioritized to ensure future research meets community needs. If HPV vaccination targets are to be achieved, equitable access must be supported for all people, including people with disability.

Common vaccination barriers included misassumptions around sexual activity, lack of vaccination recommendations from healthcare professionals in primarily healthcare-facility-based HPV vaccination programme settings, and lack of staff training/resources.

Controversies and gaps

Research and practice implications

聚焦 宫颈癌:进展、诊断 和临床表现、流行病学、治疗 方法、人工智能的作用和未来方向。

Spotlights on cervical cancer: progression, diagnosis and clinical manifestations, epidemiology, treatment approaches, role of artificial intelligence and future directions.
作者： Hesham M Tawfeek, Ahmed A H Abdellatif, Hassan Barakat
摘要中文翻译目标:宫颈癌 仍然是全世界女性疾病和死亡的主要原因,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家,这些国家获得 疫苗接种、筛查和高级 治疗 的机会有限。意义:本综述全面概述了 宫颈癌 的当前证据,涵盖其 流行病学、分子进展、临床表现、诊断策略和新兴治疗方法。它还强调将新兴技术(特别是人工智能)集成到 宫颈癌 筛查、诊断、预后评估和 治疗 规划中。此外,它还强调了精准医学、液体活检、靶向治疗、免疫治疗和基于纳米技术的药物输送系统的最新进展,同时确定了当前的挑战和未来的研究重点。主要发现:这篇评论文章指出了 诊断 早期 诊断 的最新应用技术,例如生物传感器可以克服免疫组织化学和生化检测的局限性。治疗性 疫苗 也已被开发作为治疗药物和外科手术的替代品,用于预防 宫颈癌。此外,靶向递送和纳米技术在肿瘤诊断、研究和管理以及HPV 疫苗的递送中的应用。人工智能 (AI) 在早期检测 HPV 感染和通过 AI 增强型机器人系统进行手术规划管理 宫颈癌 方面已展现出巨大的潜力。结论:宫颈癌 研究应侧重于精准医学,整合最新的人工智能模型、先进的筛查技术、免疫疗法和全球公共卫生举措。结合这些方法可以改善早期肿瘤检测,优化 治疗 结果,减轻疾病负担,并有助于消除 宫颈癌。

Spotlights on cervical cancer: progression, diagnosis and clinical manifestations, epidemiology, treatment approaches, role of artificial intelligence and future directions.

Authors: Hesham M Tawfeek, Ahmed A H Abdellatif, Hassan Barakat
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: Cervical cancer remains a major cause of illness and death among women worldwide, especially in low- and middle-income countries, where access to vaccination, screening, and advanced treatment is limited. SIGNIFICANCE: This review offers a comprehensive overview of the current evidence on cervical cancer, covering its epidemiology, molecular progression, clinical manifestations, diagnostic strategies, and emerging therapeutic approaches. It also emphasizes the integration of emerging technologies, particularly artificial intelligence, into cervical cancer screening, diagnosis, prognostic assessment, and treatment planning. In addition, it highlights recent advances in precision medicine, liquid biopsy, targeted therapies, immunotherapy, and nanotechnology-based drug delivery systems, while identifying current challenges and future research priorities. KEY FINDINGS: This review article pointed out the recent applied technologies for early diagnosis of cervical cancer e.g. biosensors which can overcome the limitations of immunohistochemistry and biochemical based assays. Therapeutic vaccines have also been developed as an alternative to therapeutic drugs and surgical operations for the prevention of cervical cancer. In addition, targeted delivery and nanotechnology applications in tumor diagnosis, investigation, and management as well as the delivery of HPV vaccines. Artificial intelligence (AI) has demonstrated considerable potential for the early detection of HPV infections and management of cervical cancer through surgical planning via AI-enhanced robotic systems. CONCLUSIONS: Cervical cancer research should focus on precision medicine, integrating recent models of artificial intelligence, advanced screening technologies, immunotherapy, and global public health initiatives. Combining these approaches could lead to improving early tumor detection, optimizing treatment outcomes, reducing disease burden, and contributing to eliminating cervical cancer.

Scope and question

Consensus and conclusions
Artificial intelligence (AI) has demonstrated considerable potential for the early detection of HPV infections and management of cervical cancer through surgical planning via AI-enhanced robotic systems. Therapeutic vaccines have also been developed as an alternative to therapeutic drugs and surgical operations for the prevention of cervical cancer. Combining these approaches could lead to improving early tumor detection, optimizing treatment outcomes, reducing disease burden, and contributing to eliminating cervical cancer.
Controversies and gaps
In addition, it highlights recent advances in precision medicine, liquid biopsy, targeted therapies, immunotherapy, and nanotechnology-based drug delivery systems, while identifying current challenges and future research priorities.
Research and practice implications
Cervical cancer research should focus on precision medicine, integrating recent models of artificial intelligence, advanced screening technologies, immunotherapy, and global public health initiatives.

头颈癌中的驱动蛋白超家族蛋白:机制、临床意义和治疗潜力。

Kinesin superfamily proteins in head and neck cancer: Mechanisms, clinical implications, and therapeutic potential.
作者： Tareq Nayef AlRamadneh, Renuka Jyothi S, Priya Priyadarshini Nayak, Laith Saheb, Amaal Mohammed Ali, Vimal Arora, Ashish Singh Chauhan, Gunjan Garg, Saeed Niazi Vahdati
摘要中文翻译头颈癌,特别是头颈鳞状细胞癌 (HNSCC),仍然是癌症相关发病率和 死亡率 的重要原因。尽管手术、放疗、化疗、靶向治疗和免疫治疗改善了疾病管理,但晚期或复发性疾病患者的预后仍然较差。越来越多的证据表明,驱动蛋白超家族蛋白(KIF)是一种基于微管的分子马达,参与​​细胞内运输和染色体动力学,在头颈癌中发挥着重要作用。本综述探讨了头颈癌中 KIF 失调的当前证据,特别强调其对肿瘤生物学、调节机制、临床相关性和治疗潜力的贡献。这篇叙述性综述审查了同行评审的 KIF 参与头颈癌主要特征的临床前和临床研究,包括细胞增殖、侵袭、转移、上皮间质转化 (EMT)、癌症干性、细胞周期进展、细胞凋亡和 治疗 耐药。我们还考虑了调节机制的证据,包括非编码 RNA、泛素介导的蛋白水解和 N6-甲基腺苷 (m6A) 修饰,以及调查 KIF 作为预后生物标志物或治疗靶点的研究。现有证据支持多种 KIF 在头颈癌进展中发挥重要作用,特别是通过对细胞周期调节、存活、迁移和致癌信号传导的影响。然而,KIF 活性并不均具有致癌性。个体家庭成员的影响似乎取决于肿瘤亚型、解剖部位、分子背景和 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 状态。此外,实验研究的主导地位和相对较小的临床 队列研究 限制了当前临床证据的强度。未来的研究应使用 HPV 和亚型分层模型以及整合的转录组学、蛋白质组学和表观遗传学方法来阐明各个 KIF 的特定功能。临床相关模型和更大的独立患者 队列研究 的验证对于确定其生物标志物价值非常重要。选择性驱动蛋白抑制剂也值得进一步研究,以确定这些蛋白质是否可以有效地用于头颈癌的治疗干预。

Kinesin superfamily proteins in head and neck cancer: Mechanisms, clinical implications, and therapeutic potential.

Authors: Tareq Nayef AlRamadneh, Renuka Jyothi S, Priya Priyadarshini Nayak, Laith Saheb, Amaal Mohammed Ali, Vimal Arora, Ashish Singh Chauhan, Gunjan Garg, Saeed Niazi Vahdati
Original abstract (English)
Head and neck cancer, particularly head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC), remains a substantial cause of cancer-related morbidity and mortality. Although surgery, radiotherapy, chemotherapy, targeted therapy, and immunotherapy have improved disease management, the prognosis of patients with advanced or recurrent disease remains poor. Increasing evidence suggests that kinesin superfamily proteins (KIFs), microtubule-based molecular motors involved in intracellular transport and chromosome dynamics, have important roles in head and neck cancer. This review examines the current evidence on KIF dysregulation in head and neck cancer, with particular emphasis on their contributions to tumor biology, regulatory mechanisms, clinical relevance, and therapeutic potential. This narrative review examined peer-reviewed preclinical and clinical studies on KIF involvement in major features of head and neck cancer, including cell proliferation, invasion, metastasis, epithelial-to-mesenchymal transition (EMT), cancer stemness, cell-cycle progression, apoptosis, and treatment resistance. We also considered evidence on regulatory mechanisms, including non-coding RNAs, ubiquitin-mediated proteolysis, and N6-methyladenosine (m6A) modification, along with studies investigating KIFs as prognostic biomarkers or therapeutic targets. The available evidence supports an important role for several KIFs in head and neck cancer progression, particularly through effects on cell-cycle regulation, survival, migration, and oncogenic signaling. Nevertheless, KIF activity is not uniformly oncogenic. The effects of individual family members appear to depend on tumor subtype, anatomical site, molecular context, and human papillomavirus (HPV) status. In addition, the predominance of experimental studies and relatively small clinical cohorts limits the strength of current clinical evidence. Future studies should clarify the context-specific functions of individual KIFs using HPV- and subtype-stratified models and integrated transcriptomic, proteomic, and epigenetic approaches. Validation in clinically relevant models and larger, independent patient cohorts will be important for establishing their biomarker value. Selective kinesin inhibitors also warrant further investigation to determine whether these proteins can be effectively exploited for therapeutic intervention in head and neck cancer.

Controversies and gaps

Research and practice implications

局部晚期头颈鳞状细胞癌新辅助治疗的进展(综述)。

Advances in neoadjuvant therapy for locally advanced head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (Review).
作者： Hong You, Xinghu Qin, Shaozhi Fu
摘要中文翻译大多数头颈癌起源于口腔、咽和喉部的粘膜鳞状上皮,统称为头颈鳞状细胞癌(HNSCC)。病例管理采用多模式护理标准。原发性口腔癌的治疗方法是切除,然后进行基于顺铂的辅助放化疗 (CRT),并且针对咽或喉部疾病应用明确的同步 CRT。在医学上不适合铂类药物 人乳头瘤病毒 的 人乳头瘤病毒 阴性患者中,表皮生长因子受体导向的单克隆抗体西妥昔单抗与放疗 (RT) 同时给药。最近,免疫检查点抑制剂 (ICIs) 重新定义了复发/转移性 HNSCC 的全身治疗,产生持久的反应和生存获益。总的来说,65-70% 的患者患有 III-IVA 期局部晚期 (LA) 疾病,这种情况与治疗挑战和生活质量显着恶化相关。新辅助治疗旨在降低原发肿瘤的阶段,根除隐匿性微转移性疾病,并在确定性手术或放疗前增强局部控制,从而为器官保存提供合理的策略。当代治疗方案将紫杉烷、铂类和 5-氟尿嘧啶诱导化疗与放疗相结合,早期试验已经探索了将 ICI 纳入新辅助治疗方案的可行性和有效性。本综述总结了 LA HNSCC 新辅助治疗的 进化,特别强调新兴的基于免疫治疗的范例及其改善肿瘤和功能结果的潜力。

本综述总结了 LA HNSCC 新辅助治疗的 进化,特别强调新兴的基于免疫治疗的范例及其改善肿瘤和功能结果的潜力。
证据基础与方法
提供的证据没有明确说明审查方法、数据库、资格流程或证据基础。
共识与结论
总的来说,65-70% 的患者患有 III-IVA 期局部晚期 (LA) 疾病,这种情况与治疗挑战和生活质量显着恶化相关。
争议与缺口
当代治疗方案将紫杉烷、铂类和 5-氟尿嘧啶诱导化疗与放疗相结合,早期试验已经探索了将 ICI 纳入新辅助治疗方案的可行性和有效性。
科研与实践启示

Advances in neoadjuvant therapy for locally advanced head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (Review).

Authors: Hong You, Xinghu Qin, Shaozhi Fu
Original abstract (English)
The majority of head and neck cancers arise from the mucosal squamous epithelium of the oral cavity, pharynx and larynx, and are collectively referred to as head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC). A multimodal standard of care is applied for case management. Primary oral cavity cancers are treated with resection followed by adjuvant cisplatin‐based chemoradiotherapy (CRT), and definitive concurrent CRT is applied for pharyngeal or laryngeal disease. In human papillomavirus‐negative patients who are medically unfit for platinum treatment, the epidermal growth factor receptor‐directed monoclonal antibody cetuximab is administered concomitantly with radiotherapy (RT). More recently, immune checkpoint inhibitors (ICIs) have redefined systemic therapy for recurrent/metastatic HNSCC, yielding durable responses and a survival benefit. In total, 65‐70% of patients present with stage III‐IVA locally advanced (LA) disease, a scenario associated with therapeutic challenges and a marked deterioration in the quality of life. Neoadjuvant therapy is designed to downstage the primary tumor, eradicate occult micrometastatic disease and enhance locoregional control before definitive surgery or RT, thus offering a rational strategy for organ preservation. Contemporary regimens integrate taxane, platinum and 5‐fluorouracil induction chemotherapy with RT, and early‐phase trials have explored the feasibility and efficacy of incorporating ICIs into neoadjuvant protocols. The present review summarizes the evolution of neoadjuvant therapy for LA HNSCC, with particular emphasis on emerging immunotherapy‐based paradigms and their potential to improve oncological and functional outcomes.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The present review summarizes the evolution of neoadjuvant therapy for LA HNSCC, with particular emphasis on emerging immunotherapy‐based paradigms and their potential to improve oncological and functional outcomes.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review method, databases, eligibility process, or evidence base.
Consensus and conclusions
In total, 65‐70% of patients present with stage III‐IVA locally advanced (LA) disease, a scenario associated with therapeutic challenges and a marked deterioration in the quality of life.
Controversies and gaps
Contemporary regimens integrate taxane, platinum and 5‐fluorouracil induction chemotherapy with RT, and early‐phase trials have explored the feasibility and efficacy of incorporating ICIs into neoadjuvant protocols.
Research and practice implications
In human papillomavirus‐negative patients who are medically unfit for platinum treatment, the epidermal growth factor receptor‐directed monoclonal antibody cetuximab is administered concomitantly with radiotherapy (RT).

BNT113 加派姆单抗在 HPV16+ HNSCC 中未达到 PFS 终点 - 靶向肿瘤学

BNT113 Plus Pembrolizumab Misses PFS End Point in HPV16+ HNSCC - Targeted Oncology
新闻简报Targeted Oncology 的新闻报道讨论了一项评估 BNT113 和 Pembrolizumab 联合治疗 HPV16 阳性头颈鳞状细胞癌 (HNSCC) 患者的临床试验。该试验错过了无进展生存期(PFS)终点,表明 治疗 未达到其主要目标。该报告强调了肿瘤学领域持续研究和开发的重要性,特别是针对 HPV 相关癌症。然而,没有提供有关病例数、死亡人数或接触情况的具体细节。证据表明,试验结果是关于 治疗 肿瘤学进展和挑战的更广泛讨论的一部分。报告没有提及任何具体的应对措施或与试验结果相关的不确定性,有待进一步调查和分析。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

BNT113 Plus Pembrolizumab Misses PFS End Point in HPV16+ HNSCC - Targeted Oncology

News Brief
The news report from Targeted Oncology discusses a clinical trial evaluating the combination of BNT113 and Pembrolizumab in patients with HPV16-positive head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC). The trial missed the progression-free survival (PFS) end point, indicating that the treatment did not meet its primary objective. The report highlights the importance of continued research and development in oncology, particularly for HPV-associated cancers. However, no specific details about the number of cases, deaths, or exposures are provided. The evidence suggests that the trial results are part of a broader discussion on treatment advancements and challenges in oncology. The report does not mention any specific response measures or uncertainties related to the trial outcomes, leaving the situation open for further investigation and analysis.

高危型HPV 未涵盖的基因型在拉各斯女性中占主导地位的宫颈感染

High-Risk HPV Genotypes Not Covered by Vaccines Dominate Cervical Infections in Lagos Women
新闻简报2017 年 7 月至 2018 年 6 月在尼日利亚拉各斯进行的一项研究显示,疫苗 未涵盖的 高危型HPV 基因型,特别是 HPV-35,在 165 名性活跃女性中主导宫颈感染。这项发表在《BMC Infectious Drugs》上的研究发现,43.6% 的参与者 HPV-35 检测呈阳性,与欧洲和北美的研究相比,HPV-35 在这一人群中更为普遍。此外,34.5% 的女性患有多种 高危型HPV 感染,凸显了当前筛查方法的局限性。这项由迦勒大学 (Caleb University) 的 Oluwaseyi Sedowhe Ashaka 领导的研究强调了在具有不同 HPV 基因型分布的地区进行更广泛的分子检测和重新评估 疫苗 策略的必要性。研究结果表明,HPV-35 在宫颈疾病进展中发挥着重要作用,出现在从正常到高级别病变的所有细胞学级别中。
